            falsification_criteria="Reject if alias route remained stable or new snapshot is proven equivalent.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.ACTIVE if alias_change else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    return [h for h in hypotheses if h.support_evidence or h.opposition_evidence or h.unknowns]


def skeptic_review(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    known_facts: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    flags: List[str] = []
    alternatives: List[str] = []
    questions: List[str] = []

    if any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references):
        flags.append("ENTITY_RESOLUTION_AMBIGUOUS")
        alternatives.append("Reference may denote product, API alias, deployment, or model family rather than exact checkpoint.")

    if any(g.get("comparability_status") == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED.name for g in benchmark_groups.values()):
        flags.append("BENCHMARK_COMPARABILITY_LIMITED")
        alternatives.append("Scores may use different prompting, shots, tools, sampling, evaluator, or benchmark version.")

    if any(g.get("conflict") for g in benchmark_groups.values()):
        flags.append("BENCHMARK_SCORE_CONFLICT")
        alternatives.append("Conflict may reflect non-independent republication, protocol drift, or measurement error.")

    if any(sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} for sm in security_mappings):
        flags.append("SECURITY_LAYER_NOT_MODEL")
        alternatives.append("Issue may reside in RAG, prompt, tool authorization, output handling, or runtime policy.")

    if any(
        any(p.get("state") == "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE" for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    ):
        flags.append("INCIDENT_DEPLOYMENT_SCOPE_UNRESOLVED")
        alternatives.append("Missing deployment inventory may hide affected systems.")

    if supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        flags.append("COMMON_MODE_DEPENDENCY")
        alternatives.append("Shared dependency may be intentional, contracted, or an artifact of incomplete inventory.")

    if change_analysis.get("flags"):
        flags.extend(change_analysis["flags"])
        alternatives.append("Behavior change may be operational rollout rather than degradation.")

    if contradictions:
        flags.append("CONTRADICTIONS_PRESENT")
        alternatives.append("Contradictions may reflect stale docs, alias routing, provider updates, or source dependency.")

    questions.extend(
        [
            "Did we separate model, product, API, deployment, and agent?",
            "Is the exact version/checkpoint resolved or only an alias?",
            "Are benchmark protocols normalized?",
            "Is capability provider-claimed or independently measured?",
            "Could RAG/tools/guardrails explain observed behavior?",
            "Does security finding affect base model or surrounding system?",
            "Are multiple sources actually independent?",
            "What evidence would disprove supply-chain or incident scope claims?",
        ]
    )

    return {
        "flags": sorted(set(flags)),
        "alternative_explanations": sorted(set(alternatives)),
        "diagnostic_questions": questions,
        "review_outcome": "PARTIAL_AGREEMENT" if flags else "AGREE",
        "boundary": "Defensive intelligence only. No model theft, prompt extraction, safeguard bypass, poisoning, or harmful agent enablement.",
    }


def build_facts(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    source_independence: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[FactRecord]:
    facts: List[FactRecord] = []

    for rr in resolved_references:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"AI reference '{rr.get('reference')}' resolved to layer {rr.get('layer')} with primary id {rr.get('primary_id')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.INDEPENDENT_OBSERVATION.name if not rr.get("ambiguous") else ClaimType.HYPOTHESIS.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.SUPPORTED if not rr.get("ambiguous") else VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE,
                evidence_ids=rr.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=rr.get("resolved_ids", []),
                confidence=0.8 if not rr.get("ambiguous") else 0.45,
                limitations=["Reference resolution may be alias-dependent."],
            )
        )

    for m in models:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Model record observed: {m.canonical_name} ({m.model_id}), type={m.model_type.name}, access={m.access_type.name}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.OFFICIAL_CLAIM.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=m.evidence_ids,
                object_ids=[m.model_id],
                confidence=m.confidence,
                limitations=m.limitations,
            )
        )

    for lin in lineage:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Model lineage edge observed: {lin.get('source_model_id')} {lin.get('relation')} {lin.get('target_model_id')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.DERIVED_RESULT.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=lin.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=[lin.get("source_model_id") or "", lin.get("target_model_id") or ""],
                confidence=float(lin.get("confidence", 0.5)),
                limitations=lin.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    for br in benchmark_results:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=(
                    f"Benchmark result observed: model={br.model_id}, benchmark={br.benchmark_id}/{br.benchmark_version}, "
                    f"score={br.score}, comparability={br.comparability_status.name}."
                ),
                claim_type=ClaimType.MEASURED_RESULT.name if br.source_type in INDEPENDENT_EVAL_SOURCES else ClaimType.OFFICIAL_CLAIM.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=br.evidence_ids,
                object_ids=[br.result_id],
                confidence=br.confidence,
                limitations=br.limitations,
            )
        )

    for sm in security_mappings:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Security finding mapped to layer {sm.get('affected_layer')}; base-model impact={sm.get('base_model_impact')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.INDEPENDENT_OBSERVATION.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=[],
                object_ids=[sm.get("finding_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.7,
                limitations=["Layer mapping does not establish exploitation."],
            )
        )

    for inc in incident_props:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Incident {inc.get('incident_id')} propagation state resolved against available deployment inventory.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.ANALYST_INFERENCE.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE,
                evidence_ids=[],
                object_ids=[inc.get("incident_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.6,
                limitations=inc.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    if supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement="Common-mode AI dependencies observed across multiple deployments/applications.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.ANALYST_INFERENCE.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=[],
                object_ids=[],
                confidence=0.7,
                limitations=supply_chain.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    for est in cost_estimates:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Deterministic cost estimate computed for entity {est.get('entity_id')} using pricing {est.get('pricing_id')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.DERIVED_RESULT.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=est.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=[est.get("estimate_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.9,
                limitations=est.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    for ch in change_analysis.get("timeline", []):
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Change event observed: {ch.get('change_type')} for entity {ch.get('entity_id')} effective {ch.get('effective_at')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.OFFICIAL_CLAIM.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=ch.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=[ch.get("change_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.75,
                limitations=["Change does not automatically imply improvement or degradation."],
            )
        )

    for c in contradictions:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Contradiction: {c.contradiction_type} — {c.description}",
                claim_type=ClaimType.UNKNOWN.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.DISPUTED,
                evidence_ids=c.evidence_ids,
                object_ids=c.object_ids,
                confidence=0.75,
                limitations=["Contradictions must be preserved, not silently resolved."],
            )
        )

    facts.append(
        FactRecord(
            fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
            statement=f"Source independence state: {source_independence.get('state')}.",
            claim_type=ClaimType.ANALYST_INFERENCE.name,
            verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
            evidence_ids=[],
            object_ids=[],
            confidence=0.7,
            limitations=source_independence.get("limitations", []),
        )
    )

    facts.append(
        FactRecord(
            fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
            statement=(
                "No autonomous capability, harmful intent, model theft, safeguard bypass, or real-world attribution "
                "is established by AIINT artifact intelligence alone."
            ),
            claim_type=ClaimType.UNKNOWN.name,
            verification_state=VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE,
            evidence_ids=[],
            object_ids=[],
            confidence=0.99,
            limitations=["Consequential security, legal, governance, and attribution decisions require human review."],
        )
    )

    return facts


def build_gaps(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    cost_missing: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
) -> List[Gap]:
    gaps: List[Gap] = []

    def add_gap(description: str, importance: str, recommended_source: str, specialist: str, expected_information_value: str) -> None:
        gaps.append(
            Gap(
                gap_id=new_id("GAP"),
                description=description,
                importance=importance,
                recommended_source=recommended_source,
                specialist=specialist,
                expected_information_value=expected_information_value,
            )
        )

    if not resolved_references or any(not rr.get("primary_id") for rr in resolved_references):
        add_gap(
            "AI entity reference unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "official model card / provider API docs / authorized model inventory",
            "AIINT",
            "Establishes whether reference denotes model, API, product, deployment, or agent.",
        )

    if any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references):
        add_gap(
            "Model/API/product alias ambiguity detected.",
            "HIGH",
            "release notes / versioned endpoint metadata / deployment logs",
            "AIINT / CLOUDINT",
            "Resolves exact version/checkpoint and avoids layer confusion.",
        )

    if not any(m.model_id for m in models if m.access_type != AccessType.UNKNOWN):
        add_gap(
            "Model access type unknown.",
            "MEDIUM",
            "license text / provider docs / model repository",
            "AIINT / LEGALINT",
            "Distinguishes open weights, API-only, hosted service, and proprietary access.",
        )

    if not lineage:
        add_gap(
            "Model lineage unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "official model card / technical report / release notes",
            "AIINT",
            "Separates base, fine-tune, adapter, quantized, and distilled variants.",
        )

    if any(br.comparability_status == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED for br in benchmark_results):
        add_gap(
            "Benchmark protocol incomplete for one or more results.",
            "HIGH",
            "evaluation harness / benchmark card / reproducible submission",
            "AIINT / EVALINT",
            "Enables apples-to-apples comparison and avoids leaderboard overclaim.",
        )

    if any(sm.get("base_model_impact") in {"NOT_ESTABLISHED", "CANDIDATE_REQUIRES_VALIDATION"} for sm in security_mappings):
        add_gap(
            "Security finding layer mapping to base model unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "advisory details / affected component metadata / deployment version",
            "AIINT / VULNINT / INCIDENTINT",
            "Prevents product/API/RAG/tool issue from being misattributed to base model.",
        )

    if any(
        any(p.get("state") in {"NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE", "POTENTIALLY_AFFECTED"} for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    ):
        add_gap(
            "Incident deployment scope unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "authorized model inventory / deployment logs / provider status",
            "AIINT / CLOUDINT / INCIDENTINT",
            "Determines which applications/deployments used affected version during window.",
        )

    if not supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        add_gap(
            "Common-mode dependency analysis incomplete.",
            "MEDIUM",
            "authorized AI inventory / SBOM / inference stack metadata",
            "AIINT / SUPPLYCHAININT",
            "Identifies shared provider/model/framework/vector-store/guardrail concentration.",
        )

    if cost_missing:
        add_gap(
            "Pricing record missing or not time-aligned for cost usage.",
            "MEDIUM",
            "provider pricing page / contract / cloud billing export",
            "AIINT / FINOPS",
            "Enables deterministic cost estimation with as-of-date context.",
        )

    if change_analysis.get("flags"):
        add_gap(
            "Change/drift event requires evaluation and deployment correlation.",
            "HIGH",
            "release notes / model version logs / evaluation harness",
            "AIINT / INCIDENTINT",
            "Distinguishes intentional update from behavior/cost/safety drift.",
        )

    if contradictions:
        add_gap(
            "Evidence contradictions require source/time/version reconciliation.",
            "HIGH",
            "primary provider docs / versioned artifacts / independent evaluation",
            "AIINT",
            "Prevents alias, stale docs, or dependent sources from driving false conclusions.",
        )

    add_gap(
        "Real-world intent, maliciousness, or actor attribution unresolved.",
        "HIGH",
        "authorized incident/CTI/legal review",
        "CTI / INCIDENTINT / LEGALINT",
        "Prevents technical AI ecosystem intelligence from becoming unsupported attribution.",
    )

    return gaps


def build_graph(
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    facts: List[FactRecord],
    hypotheses: List[Hypothesis],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    gaps: List[Gap],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    nodes: Dict[str, GraphNode] = {}
    edges: List[GraphEdge] = []

    def add_node(node_id: str, node_type: str, attributes: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        nodes[node_id] = GraphNode(node_id=node_id, type=node_type, attributes=attributes)

    def add_edge(source: str, target: str, relation: str, confidence: float, evidence_ids: List[str]) -> None:
        edges.append(
            GraphEdge(
                edge_id=new_id("EDGE"),
                source_node_id=source,
                target_node_id=target,
                relation=relation,
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence_ids=sorted(set(evidence_ids)),
            )
        )

    for e in entity_index.values():
        add_node(
            f"N_ENTITY_{e.entity_id}",
            e.entity_type,
            {
                "name": e.name,
                "aliases": e.aliases,
                "confidence": e.confidence,
                "limitations": e.limitations,
            },
        )

    for r in relationships:
        src = f"N_ENTITY_{r.source_id}"
        tgt = f"N_ENTITY_{r.target_id}"
        if src not in nodes:
            add_node(src, "UNRESOLVED_ENTITY", {"entity_id": r.source_id})
        if tgt not in nodes:
            add_node(tgt, "UNRESOLVED_ENTITY", {"entity_id": r.target_id})
        add_edge(src, tgt, r.relation, r.confidence, r.evidence_ids)

    for lin in lineage:
        src = f"N_ENTITY_{lin.get('source_model_id')}"
        tgt = f"N_ENTITY_{lin.get('target_model_id')}"
        if src not in nodes:
            add_node(src, "MODEL", {"entity_id": lin.get("source_model_id")})
        if tgt not in nodes:
            add_node(tgt, "MODEL", {"entity_id": lin.get("target_model_id")})
        add_edge(src, tgt, str(lin.get("relation")), float(lin.get("confidence", 0.5)), lin.get("evidence_ids", []))

    for br in benchmark_results:
        node_id = f"N_BMRESULT_{br.result_id}"
        add_node(
            node_id,
            "BenchmarkResult",
            {
                "model_id": br.model_id,
                "benchmark_id": br.benchmark_id,
                "score": br.score,
                "comparability_status": br.comparability_status.name,
            },
        )
        if br.model_id:
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{br.model_id}", "SCORED_ON_MODEL", br.confidence, br.evidence_ids)
        if br.benchmark_id:
            if f"N_ENTITY_{br.benchmark_id}" not in nodes:
                add_node(f"N_ENTITY_{br.benchmark_id}", "BENCHMARK", {"benchmark_id": br.benchmark_id})
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{br.benchmark_id}", "USES_BENCHMARK", br.confidence, br.evidence_ids)

    for sm in security_mappings:
        node_id = f"N_SEC_{sm.get('finding_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "SecurityFinding", sm)
        if sm.get("affected_entity_id"):
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{sm.get('affected_entity_id')}", "AFFECTS", 0.7, [])

    for inc in incident_props:
        node_id = f"N_INC_{inc.get('incident_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "Incident", inc)

    for est in cost_estimates:
        node_id = f"N_COST_{est.get('estimate_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "CostEstimate", est)
        if est.get("entity_id"):
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{est.get('entity_id')}", "COST_FOR", 0.9, est.get("evidence_ids", []))

    for ch in change_analysis.get("timeline", []):
        node_id = f"N_CHG_{ch.get('change_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "ChangeEvent", ch)
        if ch.get("entity_id"):
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{ch.get('entity_id')}", "CHANGES", 0.8, ch.get("evidence_ids", []))

    for f in facts:
        add_node(f"N_FACT_{f.fact_id}", "Fact", asdict(f))

    for h in hypotheses:
        add_node(f"N_HYP_{h.id}", "Hypothesis", asdict(h))

    for c in contradictions:
        add_node(f"N_CONTRA_{c.contradiction_id}", "Contradiction", asdict(c))

    for g in gaps:
        add_node(f"N_GAP_{g.gap_id}", "Gap", asdict(g))

    return {
        "nodes": [asdict(n) for n in nodes.values()],
        "edges": [asdict(e) for e in edges],
    }


def generate_summary(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    capability: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    gaps: List[Gap],
    source_independence: Dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    lines: List[str] = []

    lines.append("=== AIINT REQUIRED ANALYST SUMMARY ===")

    lines.append("AI ENTITY / REFERENCE:")
    for rr in resolved_references:
        lines.append(f"- {rr.get('reference')} -> layer={rr.get('layer')}, primary={rr.get('primary_id')}, ambiguous={rr.get('ambiguous')}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("MODEL / FAMILY / VERSION:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(
            f"- {m.canonical_name} ({m.model_id}): family={m.family_id}, type={m.model_type.name}, "
            f"access={m.access_type.name}, context={m.context_window}, params={m.parameter_count}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("DEVELOPER / PROVIDER:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(f"- {m.model_id}: developer={m.developer_id}, providers={m.provider_ids}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("PRODUCT / API / DEPLOYMENT / AGENT SEPARATION:")
    lines.append("- References are resolved by layer; product/API/deployment/agent capability is not collapsed into base model capability.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("MODEL LINEAGE:")
    for lin in lineage:
        lines.append(f"- {lin.get('source_model_id')} {lin.get('relation')} {lin.get('target_model_id')} [{lin.get('lineage_confidence')}]")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("MODALITIES / CONTEXT / TOOLS:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(
            f"- {m.model_id}: modality={m.modality}, max_context={m.context_window}, max_output={m.max_output_tokens}, "
            f"tool_use={m.attributes.get('tool_use')}, function_calling={m.attributes.get('function_calling')}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("CAPABILITY CLAIMS vs MEASUREMENTS:")
    for cap in capability:
        lines.append(f"- {cap.get('model_id')}: claims={len(cap.get('claims', []))}, measurements={len(cap.get('measurements', []))}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("BENCHMARK COMPARABILITY:")
    for br in benchmark_results:
        lines.append(
            f"- {br.result_id}: model={br.model_id}, benchmark={br.benchmark_id}/{br.benchmark_version}, "
            f"score={br.score}, status={br.comparability_status.name}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("LICENSE / POLICY:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(f"- {m.model_id}: license={m.license_id}, policies={m.policy_ids}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("TRAINING DATA / DATASET PROVENANCE:")
    lines.append("- Training-data claims are preserved as disclosed/inferred/unknown and are not converted into exact record facts.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("SAFETY / SECURITY:")
    for sm in security_mappings:
        lines.append(
            f"- {sm.get('finding_id')}: layer={sm.get('affected_layer')}, "
            f"base_model_impact={sm.get('base_model_impact')}, issue={sm.get('issue_type')}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("AI INCIDENTS:")
    for inc in incident_props:
        lines.append(f"- {inc.get('incident_id')}: model={inc.get('model_id')}, propagation_entries={len(inc.get('deployment_propagation', []))}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("RAG / AGENT / TOOL CONTEXT:")
    lines.append("- RAG, embedding, reranker, vector DB, tool, MCP, guardrail, and monitoring dependencies are represented separately from model intrinsic capability.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("FRAMEWORK / PACKAGE / INFERENCE / HARDWARE / CLOUD:")
    if supply_chain.get("paths"):
        for path in supply_chain["paths"][:20]:
            lines.append("- " + " -> ".join(path))
    else:
        lines.append("- No supply-chain paths resolved from supplied relationships.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("COMMON-MODE DEPENDENCIES:")
    for cm in supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies", [])[:20]:
        lines.append(f"- {cm.get('dependency_type')} {cm.get('dependency_id')} shared by {cm.get('deployment_ids')}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("COST / LATENCY / THROUGHPUT:")
    for est in cost_estimates:
        lines.append(
            f"- {est.get('entity_id')}: total_cost={est.get('total_cost')} {est.get('currency')} as_of={est.get('pricing_effective_date')}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("CHANGE / DRIFT:")
    for flag in change_analysis.get("flags", []):
        lines.append(f"- {flag}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("SOURCE RELIABILITY / INDEPENDENCE:")
    lines.append(f"- Source independence state: {source_independence.get('state')}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("CONTRADICTIONS:")
    if contradictions:
        for c in contradictions:
            lines.append(f"- {c.contradiction_type}: {c.description} [{c.severity}]")
    else:
        lines.append("- None detected.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("UNKNOWN:")
    lines.append("- Exact checkpoint behind mutable API alias, if applicable.")
    lines.append("- Effective reasoning quality across full context window.")
    lines.append("- Real-world reliability, safety, and intent.")
    lines.append("- Full deployment scope and hidden routing/fallback behavior.")
    lines.append("- Threat actor, malicious intent, or exploitation status.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("NEXT ACTION:")
    lines.append("- Resolve exact model/version/checkpoint and separate API alias routing.")
    lines.append("- Retrieve official model card, system card, release notes, and license text.")
    lines.append("- Normalize benchmark protocol before comparing scores.")
    lines.append("- Map RAG/tool/guardrail/monitoring dependencies before attributing behavior to model.")
    lines.append("- Hand vulnerability applicability to VULNINT and incident scope to INCIDENTINT.")
    lines.append("- Hand package/framework deep analysis to PACKAGEINT and repository details to REPOINT.")
    lines.append("- Hand license/regulatory interpretation to LEGALINT/GRC.")
    lines.append("- Do not extract weights, harvest keys, bypass safeguards, jailbreak, poison, or deploy harmful agents.")

    return "\n".join(lines)


# ==============================================================================
# MAIN ENGINE
# ==============================================================================

class AIIntEmployee:
    """
    Defensive AIINT employee.

    Consumes lawful public/authorized AI ecosystem evidence representations.
    Does not steal models, extract weights, harvest/use API keys, bypass safeguards,
    extract confidential prompts, automate jailbreaks, poison models/data/RAG/memory,
    compromise ML pipelines/inference infrastructure, or deploy harmful autonomous agents.
    """

    def __init__(self, model_mode: str = "LOCAL_ONLY"):
        self.model_mode = model_mode.upper()
        logger.info("AIINT employee initialized in mode=%s", self.model_mode)

    def process_case(
        self,
        case_id: str,
        task_id: str,
        objective: str,
        scope: Dict[str, Any],
        evidence_input: Dict[str, Any],
        known_facts: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        known_facts = known_facts or {}
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        try:
            enforce_policy(objective, scope)
        except PolicyViolation as exc:
            return {
                "case_id": case_id,
                "task_id": task_id,
                "objective": objective,
                "status": "POLICY_BLOCKED",
                "error": str(exc),
                "privacy_flags": [
                    "AUTHORIZED_STATIC_ONLY",
                    "NO_MODEL_THEFT",
                    "NO_WEIGHT_EXTRACTION",
                    "NO_API_KEY_HARVESTING",
                    "NO_SYSTEM_PROMPT_EXTRACTION",
                    "NO_JAILBREAK_AUTOMATION",
                    "NO_POISONING_ENABLEMENT",
                ],
            }

        logger.info("Starting AIINT case=%s task=%s", case_id, task_id)

        evidences: List[EvidenceRef] = []
        entities: List[Entity] = []

        entity_specs = [
            ("ai_companies", "AI_COMPANY", "provider_docs"),
            ("model_developers", "MODEL_DEVELOPER", "official_model_card"),
            ("model_providers", "MODEL_PROVIDER", "provider_docs"),
            ("model_families", "MODEL_FAMILY", "official_model_card"),
            ("products", "PRODUCT", "provider_docs"),
            ("applications", "APPLICATION", "authorized_internal_inventory"),
            ("apis", "API", "api_docs"),
            ("deployments", "DEPLOYMENT", "authorized_internal_inventory"),
            ("agents", "AGENT", "authorized_internal_inventory"),
            ("agent_frameworks", "AGENT_FRAMEWORK", "package_registry"),
            ("orchestrators", "ORCHESTRATOR", "provider_docs"),
            ("tools", "TOOL", "provider_docs"),
            ("connectors", "CONNECTOR", "provider_docs"),
            ("mcp_servers", "MCP_SERVER", "provider_docs"),
            ("datasets", "DATASET", "dataset_card"),
            ("dataset_versions", "DATASET_VERSION", "dataset_card"),
            ("training_data_claims", "TRAINING_DATA_CLAIM", "official_model_card"),
            ("benchmarks", "BENCHMARK", "reproducible_benchmark"),
            ("evaluations", "EVALUATION", "independent_evaluator"),
            ("safety_evaluations", "SAFETY_EVALUATION", "official_system_card"),
            ("red_team_reports", "RED_TEAM_REPORT", "authorized_evaluation"),
            ("model_cards", "MODEL_CARD", "official_model_card"),
            ("system_cards", "SYSTEM_CARD", "official_system_card"),
            ("frameworks", "FRAMEWORK", "package_registry"),
            ("packages", "PACKAGE", "package_registry"),
            ("inference_engines", "INFERENCE_ENGINE", "provider_docs"),
            ("hardware", "HARDWARE", "hardware_doc"),
            ("cloud_services", "CLOUD_SERVICE", "cloud_doc"),
            ("vector_databases", "VECTOR_DATABASE", "provider_docs"),
            ("embedding_models", "EMBEDDING_MODEL", "official_model_card"),
            ("rerankers", "RERANKER", "official_model_card"),
            ("rag_systems", "RAG_SYSTEM", "authorized_internal_inventory"),
            ("guardrails", "GUARDRAIL", "provider_docs"),
            ("monitoring", "MONITORING", "authorized_internal_log"),
            ("governance_contexts", "GOVERNANCE_CONTEXT", "official_project_docs"),
            ("regulation_contexts", "REGULATION_CONTEXT", "provider_docs"),
            ("licenses", "LICENSE", "license_text"),
            ("policies", "POLICY", "provider_docs"),
        ]

        for key, etype, default_source in entity_specs:
            entities.extend(
                parse_entities(
                    evidence_input.get(key, []) or [],
                    etype,
                    case_id,
                    now,
                    evidences,
                    default_source,
                )
            )

        relationships = parse_relationships(evidence_input.get("relationships", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        models = parse_models(evidence_input.get("models", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        benchmark_results = parse_benchmark_results(evidence_input.get("benchmark_results", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        security_findings = parse_security_findings(evidence_input.get("security_findings", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        incidents = parse_incidents(evidence_input.get("incidents", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        pricing = parse_pricing(evidence_input.get("pricing", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        change_events = parse_change_events(evidence_input.get("change_events", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)
        cost_usage = parse_cost_usage(evidence_input.get("cost_usage", []) or [], case_id, now, evidences)

        (
            entity_index,
            alias_to_ids,
            rel_by_source,
            rel_by_target,
            evidence_by_id,
        ) = build_indexes(entities, models, relationships, evidences)

        resolved_references = resolve_references(evidence_input.get("ai_references", []) or [], alias_to_ids, entity_index)
        lineage = build_lineage(models, relationships, evidence_by_id)
        capability = assess_capability(models, benchmark_results)
        benchmark_groups, benchmark_conflicts = normalize_benchmark_results(benchmark_results)
        security_mappings = map_security_layers(security_findings, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index)
        incident_props = propagate_incidents(incidents, entities, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index)
        supply_chain = map_supply_chain(models, entities, relationships, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index)
        cost_estimates, cost_missing = estimate_costs(pricing, cost_usage, now)
        substitutability = assess_substitutability(models, resolved_references)
        lock_in = assess_lock_in(entities, rel_by_source, entity_index)
        change_analysis = analyze_changes(change_events)
        source_independence = analyze_source_independence(evidences)

        contradictions = detect_contradictions(
            resolved_references=resolved_references,
            alias_to_ids=alias_to_ids,
            entity_index=entity_index,
            benchmark_groups=benchmark_groups,
            benchmark_conflicts=benchmark_conflicts,
            pricing=pricing,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            change_events=change_events,
            relationships=relationships,
        )

        hypotheses = build_hypotheses(
            resolved_references=resolved_references,
            lineage=lineage,
            benchmark_groups=benchmark_groups,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            incident_props=incident_props,
            supply_chain=supply_chain,
            change_analysis=change_analysis,
            contradictions=contradictions,
            known_facts=known_facts,
        )

        skeptic = skeptic_review(
            resolved_references=resolved_references,
            benchmark_groups=benchmark_groups,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            incident_props=incident_props,
            supply_chain=supply_chain,
            change_analysis=change_analysis,
            contradictions=contradictions,
            known_facts=known_facts,
        )

        facts = build_facts(
            resolved_references=resolved_references,
            models=models,
            lineage=lineage,
            benchmark_results=benchmark_results,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            incident_props=incident_props,
            supply_chain=supply_chain,
            cost_estimates=cost_estimates,
            change_analysis=change_analysis,
            contradictions=contradictions,
            source_independence=source_independence,
        )

        gaps = build_gaps(
            resolved_references=resolved_references,
            models=models,
            lineage=lineage,
            benchmark_results=benchmark_results,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            incident_props=incident_props,
            supply_chain=supply_chain,
            cost_estimates=cost_estimates,
            cost_missing=cost_missing,
            change_analysis=change_analysis,
            contradictions=contradictions,
        )

        graph = build_graph(
            entity_index=entity_index,
            relationships=relationships,
            lineage=lineage,
            benchmark_results=benchmark_results,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            incident_props=incident_props,
            cost_estimates=cost_estimates,
            change_analysis=change_analysis,
            facts=facts,
            hypotheses=hypotheses,
            contradictions=contradictions,
            gaps=gaps,
        )

        analyst_summary = generate_summary(
            resolved_references=resolved_references,
            models=models,
            lineage=lineage,
            benchmark_results=benchmark_results,
            capability=capability,
            security_mappings=security_mappings,
            incident_props=incident_props,
            supply_chain=supply_chain,
            cost_estimates=cost_estimates,
            change_analysis=change_analysis,
            contradictions=contradictions,
            gaps=gaps,
            source_independence=source_independence,
        )

        recommended_next_actions = [
            {
                "action": "Resolve exact model/version/checkpoint and separate API alias, product, deployment, and agent layers.",
                "reason": "Mutable aliases and product wrappers can obscure artifact identity and behavior provenance.",

            },
            {

        ]

        specialist_handoffs = [

        result: Dict[str, Any] = {

                "hypotheses": [asdict(h) for h in hypotheses],
                "skeptic_review": skeptic,
            },
        }

        logger.info(
            "AIINT case completed. references=%s models=%s benchmarks=%s security_findings=%s incidents=%s",
            len(resolved_references),
            len(models),
            len(benchmark_results),
            len(security_findings),
            len(incidents),
        )

        return result

class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


PROHIBITED_SCOPE_FLAGS = (
    "allow_model_theft",
    "allow_weight_extraction",
    "allow_api_key_harvesting",
    "allow_stolen_api_key_use",
    "allow_system_prompt_extraction",
    "allow_safeguard_bypass",
    "allow_jailbreak_automation",
    "allow_training_data_theft",
    "allow_private_dataset_reconstruction",
    "allow_memorized_personal_data_extraction",
    "allow_model_poisoning",
    "allow_dataset_poisoning",
    "allow_rag_poisoning",
    "allow_agent_memory_poisoning",
    "allow_malicious_model_artifact",
    "allow_model_repository_compromise",
    "allow_ml_pipeline_compromise",
    "allow_inference_server_compromise",
    "allow_gpu_cloud_attack",
    "allow_harmful_agent_deployment",
    "allow_deepfake_abuse_pipeline",
    "allow_unauthorized_biometric_surveillance",
    "allow_billing_circumvention",
    "allow_rate_limit_circumvention",
    "allow_unauthorized_model_access",
)

PROHIBITED_OBJECTIVE_KEYWORDS = (
    "steal model",
    "extract weights",
    "bypass model access",
    "stolen api key",
    "harvest api key",
    "extract system prompt",
    "jailbreak automation",
    "remove safety controls",
    "harmful deployment",
    "exfiltrate training data",
    "reconstruct private dataset",
    "memorized personal information",
    "poison model",
    "poison dataset",
    "poison rag",
    "poison memory",
    "malicious model",
    "compromise model repository",
    "compromise ml pipeline",
    "compromise inference server",
    "attack gpu",
    "attack cloud",
    "harmful autonomous agent",
    "deepfake abuse",
    "biometric surveillance",
)


def enforce_policy(objective: str, scope: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if not isinstance(scope, dict):
        raise PolicyViolation("POLICY_BLOCKED: scope must be a dictionary.")

    mode = str(scope.get("mode", "AUTHORIZED_STATIC")).upper()
    allowed_modes = {
        "AUTHORIZED_STATIC",
        "AUTHORIZED_READONLY",
        "AUTHORIZED_EVALUATION_INGEST",
    }

    if mode not in allowed_modes:
        raise PolicyViolation(
            "POLICY_BLOCKED: AIINT requires lawful public/authorized static or read-only intelligence mode."
        )

    if mode == "AUTHORIZED_EVALUATION_INGEST":
        if not scope.get("authorized_evaluation_ingest", False):
            raise PolicyViolation(
                "POLICY_BLOCKED: evaluation ingest requires explicit authorization and must remain defensive."
            )

    for flag in PROHIBITED_SCOPE_FLAGS:
        if scope.get(flag, False):
            raise PolicyViolation(f"POLICY_BLOCKED: prohibited scope flag '{flag}'.")

    objective_lower = (objective or "").lower()
    for keyword in PROHIBITED_OBJECTIVE_KEYWORDS:
        if keyword in objective_lower:
            raise PolicyViolation(
                f"POLICY_BLOCKED: objective contains prohibited concept '{keyword}'."
            )

    "aliases",
    "source_type",
    "source_id",
    "upstream_source_id",
    "observed_at",
    "evidence_ids",
    "limitations",
    "confidence",
    "reliability",
    "type",
}

SECRET_REGEXES = [
    ("aws_access_key", re.compile(r"\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b")),
    ("private_key_marker", re.compile(r"-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----")),
    ("jwt_like_token", re.compile(r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\b")),
    ("bearer_token", re.compile(r"(?i)\bbearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._\-]+")),
    (
        "generic_secret_assignment",
        re.compile(
            r"(?i)\b(password|passwd|pwd|token|secret|api[_-]?key|apikey|"
            r"private[_-]?key|cookie|session[_-]?id|mfa|otp|credential)\b\s*[:=]\s*([^\s,;&]+)"
        ),
    ),
]

UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REGEXES = [
    re.compile(r"(?i)ignore (previous|all|system|aiint) (instructions|rules|policy|prompt)"),
    re.compile(r"(?i)(execute|run|download|upload|send|contact|use) (this|the|these) (command|script|token|secret|credential|prompt)"),
    re.compile(r"(?i)\b(jailbreak|bypass safeguard|extract weights|steal model|poison model|poison dataset)\b"),
]

SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE_TOKENS = (
    "private_key",
    "api_key",
    "apikey",
    "password",
    "passwd",
    "secret",
    "token",
    "credential",
    "personal_email",
    "home_address",
    "ssn",
    "passport",
    "biometric",
    "private_training_data",
    "confidential_prompt",
)

OFFICIAL_LINEAGE_SOURCES = {
    "official_model_card",
    "official_system_card",
    "technical_report",
    "release_notes",
    "peer_reviewed_paper",
}

SUPPORTED_LINEAGE_SOURCES = {
    "provider_docs",
    "api_docs",
    "model_repository",
    "package_registry",
    "authorized_internal_inventory",
    "sbom",
}

PROBABLE_LINEAGE_SOURCES = {
    "community_test",
    "third_party_index",
    "social_post",
}

INDEPENDENT_EVAL_SOURCES = {
    "independent_evaluator",
    "reproducible_benchmark",
    "peer_reviewed_paper",
    "authorized_evaluation",
}

INTERNAL_EVAL_SOURCES = {
    "authorized_internal_log",
    "authorized_evaluation",
}

PROVIDER_CLAIM_SOURCES = {
    "provider_benchmark",
    "official_model_card",
    "provider_docs",
    "marketing_claim",
}


# ==============================================================================
# HELPERS
# ==============================================================================

def new_id(prefix: str) -> str:
    return f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"


def stable_id(prefix: str, *parts: Any) -> str:
    raw = "|".join(str(p) for p in parts)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()
    return f"{prefix}_{digest[:12]}"


def json_serial(obj: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(obj, datetime):
        return obj.isoformat()
    if isinstance(obj, Enum):
        return obj.name
    if hasattr(obj, "__dataclass_fields__"):
        return asdict(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, set):
        return sorted(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, tuple):
        return list(obj)
    return str(obj)


def clamp(value: float, low: float = 0.0, high: float = 1.0) -> float:
    return max(low, min(high, value))


def enum_from_name(enum_cls: Any, name: Any, default: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(name, enum_cls):
        return name
    try:
        return enum_cls[str(name).upper()]
    except Exception:
        return default


def parse_dt(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if value is None:
        return None

    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        if value.tzinfo is None:
            return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)

    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text:
        return None

    text = text.replace("Z", "+00:00")

    try:
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(text)
        if dt.tzinfo is None:
            dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
        pass

    for fmt in (
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z",
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f%z",
        "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
        "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M",
        "%Y-%m-%d",
    ):
        try:
            dt = datetime.strptime(text, fmt)
            if dt.tzinfo is None:
                dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        except ValueError:
            continue

    return None


def to_int(value: Any) -> Optional[int]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return int(value)
    except Exception:
        return None


def to_float(value: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return float(value)
    except Exception:
        return None


def normalize_identifier(value: Any) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(value or "").strip().lower())


def process_text(text: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Optional[str], List[str]]:
    if text is None:
        return None, []

    s = str(text)
    flags: List[str] = []

    for _, rx in SECRET_REGEXES:
        if rx.search(s):
            s = rx.sub("[REDACTED_SECRET]", s)
            flags.append("SECRET_REDACTED")

    for rx in UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REGEXES:
        if rx.search(s):
            s = rx.sub("[UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REDACTED]", s)
            flags.append("UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REDACTED")

    return s, flags


def sanitize_mapping(
    mapping: Dict[str, Any],
    context: str = "attributes",
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    flags: List[str] = []

    for key, value in (mapping or {}).items():
        lk = str(key).lower()

        if any(token in lk for token in SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE_TOKENS):
            out[key] = "[REDACTED_SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE]"
            flags.append(f"SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE_REDACTED:{context}.{key}")
            continue

        if isinstance(value, str):
            red, f = process_text(value)
            out[key] = red
            flags.extend(f)
        elif isinstance(value, dict):
            sub, f = sanitize_mapping(value, f"{context}.{key}")
            out[key] = sub
            flags.extend(f)
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            lst: List[Any] = []
            for idx, item in enumerate(value):
                if isinstance(item, str):
                    red, f = process_text(item)
                    lst.append(red)
                    flags.extend(f)
                elif isinstance(item, dict):
                    sub, f = sanitize_mapping(item, f"{context}.{key}[{idx}]")
                    lst.append(sub)
                    flags.extend(f)
                else:
                    lst.append(item)
            out[key] = lst
        elif isinstance(value, set):
            out[key] = sorted(value)
        elif isinstance(value, tuple):
            out[key] = list(value)
        else:
            out[key] = value

    return out, flags


def make_evidence(
    item: Dict[str, Any],
    case_id: str,
    kind: str,
    now: datetime,
    default_source_type: str = "unknown",
) -> EvidenceRef:
    source_type = str(item.get("source_type") or default_source_type)
    reliability = float(item.get("reliability", SOURCE_RELIABILITY.get(source_type, 0.20)))

    observed_at = parse_dt(
        item.get("observed_at")
        or item.get("date")
        or item.get("released_at")
        or item.get("release_date")
        or item.get("published_at")
        or item.get("disclosure_date")
        or item.get("effective_date")
        or item.get("effective_at")
        or item.get("announced_at")
        or item.get("created_at")
        or item.get("first_seen")
        or item.get("last_seen")
    )

    limitations: List[str] = list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])

    if observed_at is None:
        limitations.append("OBSERVATION_TIME_UNRESOLVED")

    if source_type in {"marketing_claim", "social_post", "anonymous_claim", "unknown"}:
        limitations.append("LOWER_SOURCE_AUTHORITY")

    ev = EvidenceRef(
        evidence_id=new_id("EVID"),
        case_id=case_id,
        source_id=str(item.get("source_id") or "UNKNOWN_SOURCE"),
        upstream_source_id=str(item.get("upstream_source_id") or item.get("source_id") or "UNKNOWN_UPSTREAM"),
        source_type=source_type,
        kind=kind,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        reliability=clamp(reliability),
        limitations=sorted(set(limitations)),
    )

    item.setdefault("evidence_ids", [])
    item["evidence_ids"].append(ev.evidence_id)

    item.setdefault("confidence", ev.reliability)
    item.setdefault("limitations", [])
    item["limitations"] = sorted(set(list(item["limitations"]) + ev.limitations))

    return ev


def default_entity_limitations(entity_type: str) -> List[str]:
    t = entity_type.upper()
    base: List[str] = []

    if t == "API":
        base.append("API alias may be mutable and may route to different snapshots or regional variants.")
    elif t == "PRODUCT":
        base.append("Product capability may include retrieval, tools, guardrails, UI, and workflow logic beyond the model.")
    elif t == "APPLICATION":
        base.append("Application behavior depends on prompts, tools, data access, and orchestration.")
    elif t == "DEPLOYMENT":
        base.append("Deployment behavior may differ from base model due quantization, system prompt, RAG, tools, and policy.")
    elif t == "AGENT":
        base.append("Agent capability derives from model, tools, permissions, memory, orchestrator, and environment.")
    elif t == "DATASET":
        base.append("Dataset provenance, filtering, deduplication, and training use may be partially disclosed.")
    elif t == "BENCHMARK":
        base.append("Benchmark scores are protocol, version, prompting, tool, sampling, and date specific.")
    elif t == "LICENSE":
        base.append("License text requires legal interpretation for consequential deployment or redistribution.")
    elif t == "GUARDRAIL":
        base.append("Guardrail presence does not prove effectiveness without evaluation evidence.")
    elif t == "MONITORING":
        base.append("Observability coverage may be partial and version-specific.")

    return base


# ==============================================================================
# PARSERS
# ==============================================================================

def parse_entities(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    entity_type: str,
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
    default_source_type: str = "provider_docs",
) -> List[Entity]:
    out: List[Entity] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, entity_type.lower(), now, default_source_type)
        evidences.append(ev)

        entity_id = str(
            item.get("entity_id")
            or item.get("id")
            or stable_id(entity_type, item.get("name") or new_id("ENT"))
        )

        attributes, flags = sanitize_mapping(
            {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in META_KEYS},
            f"{entity_type.lower()}.attributes",
        )

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + default_entity_limitations(entity_type)
                + flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            Entity(
                entity_id=entity_id,
                entity_type=entity_type.upper(),
                name=item.get("name"),
                aliases=[str(x) for x in item.get("aliases", []) or []],
                attributes=attributes,
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                privacy_flags=flags,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_relationships(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Relationship]:
    out: List[Relationship] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "relationship", now, "provider_docs")
        evidences.append(ev)

        attributes, flags = sanitize_mapping(
            {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in META_KEYS},
            "relationship.attributes",
        )

        out.append(
            Relationship(
                relationship_id=str(item.get("relationship_id") or stable_id("REL", item.get("source_id"), item.get("target_id"), item.get("relation"))),
                source_id=str(item.get("source_id") or ""),
                target_id=str(item.get("target_id") or ""),
                relation=str(item.get("relation") or "RELATED_TO").upper(),
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                valid_from=parse_dt(item.get("valid_from")),
                valid_to=parse_dt(item.get("valid_to")),
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_models(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[ModelObject]:
    out: List[ModelObject] = []

    model_meta_keys = META_KEYS | {
        "model_id",
        "canonical_name",
        "family_id",
        "developer_id",
        "provider_ids",
        "model_type",
        "access_type",
        "architecture_claim",
        "parameter_count",
        "active_parameter_count",
        "modality",
        "context_window",
        "max_output_tokens",
        "tokenizer",
        "release_date",
        "knowledge_cutoff_claim",
        "license_id",
        "policy_ids",
        "repository",
        "model_card",
        "system_card",
        "base_model_id",
        "fine_tuned_from",
        "distilled_from",
        "quantized_from",
        "adapter_ids",
        "capability_claims",
        "evaluation_result_ids",
        "safety_context",
        "availability",
    }

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "model", now, "official_model_card")
        evidences.append(ev)

        capability_claims: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        claim_flags: List[str] = []

        for claim in item.get("capability_claims", []) or []:
            if isinstance(claim, str):
                red, f = process_text(claim)
                claim_flags.extend(f)
                capability_claims.append(
                    {
                        "claim": red,
                        "claim_type": CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED.name,
                        "evidence_ids": [ev.evidence_id],
                    }
                )
            elif isinstance(claim, dict):
                sanitized, f = sanitize_mapping(claim, "model.capability_claim")
                claim_flags.extend(f)
                sanitized.setdefault("claim_type", CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED.name)
                sanitized.setdefault("evidence_ids", [ev.evidence_id])
                capability_claims.append(sanitized)

        safety_context, safety_flags = sanitize_mapping(item.get("safety_context", {}) or {}, "model.safety_context")
        attributes, attr_flags = sanitize_mapping(
            {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in model_meta_keys},
            "model.attributes",
        )

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Model metadata may be provider-claimed and not independently verified.",
                    "Open weights does not automatically mean open source.",
                    "Parameter count is not a capability score.",
                    "Maximum context window is not equivalent to effective reasoning quality across full context.",
                    "Model card is not independent verification.",
                ]
                + claim_flags
                + safety_flags
                + attr_flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            ModelObject(
                model_id=str(item.get("model_id") or stable_id("MODEL", item.get("canonical_name") or item.get("name"))),
                canonical_name=item.get("canonical_name") or item.get("name"),
                family_id=item.get("family_id"),
                developer_id=item.get("developer_id"),
                provider_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("provider_ids", []) or []],
                model_type=enum_from_name(ModelType, item.get("model_type"), ModelType.UNKNOWN),
                access_type=enum_from_name(AccessType, item.get("access_type"), AccessType.UNKNOWN),
                architecture_claim=item.get("architecture_claim"),
                parameter_count=to_int(item.get("parameter_count")),
                active_parameter_count=to_int(item.get("active_parameter_count")),
                modality=[str(x) for x in item.get("modality", []) or []],
                context_window=to_int(item.get("context_window")),
                max_output_tokens=to_int(item.get("max_output_tokens")),
                tokenizer=item.get("tokenizer"),
                release_date=parse_dt(item.get("release_date")),
                knowledge_cutoff_claim=item.get("knowledge_cutoff_claim"),
                license_id=item.get("license_id"),
                policy_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("policy_ids", []) or []],
                repository=item.get("repository"),
                model_card=item.get("model_card"),
                system_card=item.get("system_card"),
                base_model_id=item.get("base_model_id"),
                fine_tuned_from=item.get("fine_tuned_from"),
                distilled_from=item.get("distilled_from"),
                quantized_from=item.get("quantized_from"),
                adapter_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("adapter_ids", []) or []],
                aliases=[str(x) for x in item.get("aliases", []) or []],
                capability_claims=capability_claims,
                evaluation_result_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("evaluation_result_ids", []) or []],
                safety_context=safety_context,
                availability=enum_from_name(ModelAvailability, item.get("availability"), ModelAvailability.UNKNOWN),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_benchmark_results(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[BenchmarkResult]:
    out: List[BenchmarkResult] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "benchmark_result", now, "provider_benchmark")
        evidences.append(ev)

        tools = item.get("tools")
        tools_list: Optional[List[str]] = None
        if tools is not None:
            tools_list = [str(x) for x in tools or []]

        protocol_fields = [
            item.get("benchmark_id"),
            item.get("benchmark_version"),
            item.get("metric"),
            item.get("prompting"),
            item.get("shots"),
            tools,
            item.get("sampling"),
            item.get("evaluator"),
            item.get("date") or item.get("published_at"),
        ]

        protocol_complete = all(x is not None for x in protocol_fields)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Benchmark score is not real-world performance proof.",
                    "Scores are comparable only under normalized protocol, version, prompting, tools, sampling, evaluator, and date context.",
                    "Provider-reported benchmark is not independent evaluation.",
                ]
            )
        )

        out.append(
            BenchmarkResult(
                result_id=str(item.get("result_id") or stable_id("BMRES", item.get("model_id"), item.get("benchmark_id"), item.get("score"), ev.source_id)),
                model_id=item.get("model_id"),
                model_version=item.get("model_version"),
                benchmark_id=item.get("benchmark_id"),
                benchmark_version=item.get("benchmark_version"),
                score=to_float(item.get("score")),
                metric=item.get("metric"),
                prompting=item.get("prompting"),
                shots=to_int(item.get("shots")),
                tools=tools_list,
                sampling=item.get("sampling"),
                evaluator=item.get("evaluator"),
                source_type=str(item.get("source_type") or ev.source_type),
                independence_group=str(item.get("independence_group") or ev.upstream_source_id),
                date=parse_dt(item.get("date") or item.get("published_at")),
                protocol_complete=protocol_complete,
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_security_findings(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
    out: List[SecurityFinding] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "security_finding", now, "security_advisory")
        evidences.append(ev)

        attributes, flags = sanitize_mapping(
            {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in META_KEYS},
            "security_finding.attributes",
        )

        affected_type = str(item.get("affected_entity_type") or "unknown").lower()

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Security issue class is not verified exploitation.",
                    "Product/API/deployment finding does not automatically establish base model vulnerability.",
                    "Mitigation guidance is defensive and does not provide exploit procedures.",
                ]
                + flags
            )
        )

        if affected_type in {"product", "application", "api", "deployment", "agent"}:
            limitations.append("Affected layer is not base model; base-model impact remains unresolved.")

        out.append(
            SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(item.get("finding_id") or stable_id("SEC", item.get("title"), item.get("affected_entity_id"), item.get("affected_version"))),
                title=item.get("title"),
                issue_type=enum_from_name(SecurityIssueType, item.get("issue_type"), SecurityIssueType.UNKNOWN),
                affected_entity_type=affected_type,
                affected_entity_id=item.get("affected_entity_id"),
                affected_version=item.get("affected_version"),
                deployment_context=item.get("deployment_context"),
                disclosure_date=parse_dt(item.get("disclosure_date")),
                severity_context=item.get("severity_context"),
                exploitability_context=item.get("exploitability_context"),
                mitigation=item.get("mitigation"),
                fixed_version=item.get("fixed_version"),
                verification_state=enum_from_name(VerificationState, item.get("verification_state"), VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_incidents(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Incident]:
    out: List[Incident] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "incident", now, "ai_incident_database")
        evidences.append(ev)

        attributes, flags = sanitize_mapping(
            {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in META_KEYS},
            "incident.attributes",
        )

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Incident claim is not verified root cause.",
                    "Provider outage does not prove every customer was impacted.",
                    "Model incident does not prove every deployment used the affected version.",
                ]
                + flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            Incident(
                incident_id=str(item.get("incident_id") or stable_id("INC", item.get("system_id"), item.get("date"), item.get("incident_type"))),
                system_id=item.get("system_id"),
                model_id=item.get("model_id"),
                provider_id=item.get("provider_id"),
                date=parse_dt(item.get("date")),
                incident_type=enum_from_name(IncidentType, item.get("incident_type"), IncidentType.UNKNOWN),
                impact=item.get("impact"),
                affected_versions=[str(x) for x in item.get("affected_versions", []) or []],
                cause_claim=item.get("cause_claim"),
                official_status=item.get("official_status"),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_pricing(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Pricing]:
    out: List[Pricing] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "pricing", now, "pricing_page")
        evidences.append(ev)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Pricing is time-specific and may change.",
                    "Token price is not total deployment cost.",
                ]
            )
        )

        out.append(
            Pricing(
                pricing_id=str(item.get("pricing_id") or stable_id("PRICE", item.get("entity_id"), item.get("effective_date"))),
                entity_id=item.get("entity_id"),
                currency=item.get("currency"),
                input_token_price_per_million=to_float(item.get("input_token_price_per_million")),
                output_token_price_per_million=to_float(item.get("output_token_price_per_million")),
                cached_token_price_per_million=to_float(item.get("cached_token_price_per_million")),
                effective_date=parse_dt(item.get("effective_date")),
                expiration_date=parse_dt(item.get("expiration_date")),
                source=item.get("source") or ev.source_id,
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_change_events(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[ChangeEvent]:
    out: List[ChangeEvent] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "change_event", now, "release_notes")
        evidences.append(ev)

        old_state, old_flags = sanitize_mapping({"value": item.get("old_state")}, "change.old_state")
        new_state, new_flags = sanitize_mapping({"value": item.get("new_state")}, "change.new_state")

        attributes, attr_flags = sanitize_mapping(
            {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in META_KEYS},
            "change.attributes",
        )

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Change is not automatically improvement.",
                    "Alias route changes may affect reproducibility and auditability.",
                ]
                + old_flags
                + new_flags
                + attr_flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            ChangeEvent(
                change_id=str(item.get("change_id") or stable_id("CHG", item.get("entity_id"), item.get("change_type"), item.get("effective_at"))),
                entity_id=item.get("entity_id"),
                change_type=enum_from_name(ChangeType, item.get("change_type"), ChangeType.UNKNOWN),
                old_state=old_state.get("value"),
                new_state=new_state.get("value"),
                effective_at=parse_dt(item.get("effective_at")),
                announced_at=parse_dt(item.get("announced_at")),
                source=item.get("source") or ev.source_id,
                impact_candidate=item.get("impact_candidate"),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_cost_usage(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "cost_usage", now, "authorized_internal_log")
        evidences.append(ev)

        sanitized, flags = sanitize_mapping(item, "cost_usage")
        sanitized["evidence_ids"] = [ev.evidence_id]
        sanitized["limitations"] = sorted(set(list(item.get("limitations", []) or []) + flags))
        out.append(sanitized)

    return out


# ==============================================================================
# ANALYSIS
# ==============================================================================

def build_indexes(
    entities: List[Entity],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> Tuple[
    Dict[str, Entity],
    Dict[str, Set[str]],
    Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    Dict[str, EvidenceRef],
]:
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity] = {}

    for e in entities:
        entity_index[e.entity_id] = e

    for m in models:
        if m.model_id not in entity_index:
            entity_index[m.model_id] = Entity(
                entity_id=m.model_id,
                entity_type="MODEL",
                name=m.canonical_name,
                aliases=m.aliases,
                attributes={
                    "model_type": m.model_type.name,
                    "access_type": m.access_type.name,
                    "family_id": m.family_id,
                    "developer_id": m.developer_id,
                    "provider_ids": m.provider_ids,
                    "license_id": m.license_id,
                    "context_window": m.context_window,
                    "parameter_count": m.parameter_count,
                },
                evidence_ids=m.evidence_ids,
                confidence=m.confidence,
                limitations=m.limitations,
            )

    alias_to_ids: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)

    for e in entity_index.values():
        if e.name:
            alias_to_ids[normalize_identifier(e.name)].add(e.entity_id)
        for alias in e.aliases:
            alias_to_ids[normalize_identifier(alias)].add(e.entity_id)
        alias_to_ids[normalize_identifier(e.entity_id)].add(e.entity_id)

    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)

    for r in relationships:
        rel_by_source[r.source_id].append(r)
        rel_by_target[r.target_id].append(r)

    evidence_by_id = {ev.evidence_id: ev for ev in evidences}

    return entity_index, alias_to_ids, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, evidence_by_id


def resolve_references(
    references: List[Any],
    alias_to_ids: Dict[str, Set[str]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for ref in references or []:
        if isinstance(ref, str):
            query = ref
            expected_type = None
        elif isinstance(ref, dict):
            query = str(ref.get("reference") or ref.get("name") or ref.get("id") or "")
            expected_type = ref.get("expected_type")
        else:
            query = str(ref)
            expected_type = None

        norm = normalize_identifier(query)
        candidates = set(alias_to_ids.get(norm, set()))

        if expected_type:
            et = str(expected_type).upper()
            candidates = {cid for cid in candidates if entity_index.get(cid) and entity_index[cid].entity_type == et}

        resolved_ids = sorted(candidates)
        primary_id = resolved_ids[0] if resolved_ids else None
        layer = entity_index[primary_id].entity_type if primary_id in entity_index else "UNRESOLVED"

        out.append(
            {
                "reference": query,
                "expected_type": expected_type,
                "resolved_ids": resolved_ids,
                "primary_id": primary_id,
                "layer": layer,
                "ambiguous": len(resolved_ids) > 1,
                "evidence_ids": entity_index[primary_id].evidence_ids if primary_id in entity_index else [],
                "limitations": [
                    "Reference resolution is alias-based and may be ambiguous.",
                    "API/product/model/deployment/agent layers must remain separate.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def lineage_confidence_from_evidence(
    evidence_ids: List[str],
    evidence_by_id: Dict[str, EvidenceRef],
) -> LineageConfidence:
    types = {evidence_by_id[eid].source_type for eid in evidence_ids if eid in evidence_by_id}

    if types & OFFICIAL_LINEAGE_SOURCES:
        return LineageConfidence.OFFICIAL
    if types & SUPPORTED_LINEAGE_SOURCES:
        return LineageConfidence.SUPPORTED
    if types & PROBABLE_LINEAGE_SOURCES:
        return LineageConfidence.PROBABLE
    return LineageConfidence.UNKNOWN


def build_lineage(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    evidence_by_id: Dict[str, EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: Set[Tuple[str, str, str]] = set()

    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in relationships:
        rel_by_source[r.source_id].append(r)

    lineage_relations = {
        "BASE_MODEL_OF",
        "FINE_TUNED_FROM",
        "DISTILLED_FROM",
        "QUANTIZED_FROM",
        "USES_ADAPTER",
        "VERSION_OF",
        "DERIVED_FROM",
    }

    def add_edge(source: str, target: str, relation: str, confidence: float, evidence_ids: List[str], source_obj: str) -> None:
        if not source or not target:
            return
        key = (source, target, relation)
        if key in seen:
            return
        seen.add(key)
