        out.append(
            {
                "lineage_id": new_id("LIN"),
                "source_model_id": source,
                "target_model_id": target,
                "relation": relation,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "lineage_confidence": lineage_confidence_from_evidence(evidence_ids, evidence_by_id).name,
                "evidence_ids": sorted(set(evidence_ids)),
                "source_object": source_obj,
                "limitations": [
                    "Lineage confidence reflects evidence strength, not behavioral equivalence.",
                    "Fine-tune/distill/quantize relationships do not imply capability or safety equivalence.",
                ],
            }
        )

    for m in models:
        if m.base_model_id:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.base_model_id, "BASED_ON", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        if m.fine_tuned_from:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.fine_tuned_from, "FINE_TUNED_FROM", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        if m.distilled_from:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.distilled_from, "DISTILLED_FROM", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        if m.quantized_from:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.quantized_from, "QUANTIZED_FROM", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        for adapter_id in m.adapter_ids:
            add_edge(m.model_id, adapter_id, "USES_ADAPTER", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)

        for r in rel_by_source.get(m.model_id, []):
            if r.relation in lineage_relations:
                add_edge(r.source_id, r.target_id, r.relation, r.confidence, r.evidence_ids + m.evidence_ids, r.relationship_id)

    return out


def assess_capability(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    results_by_model: Dict[str, List[BenchmarkResult]] = defaultdict(list)

    for br in benchmark_results:
        if br.model_id:
            results_by_model[br.model_id].append(br)

    for m in models:
        claims: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for claim in m.capability_claims:
            claim_type = str(claim.get("claim_type") or CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED.name).upper()
            claims.append(
                {
                    "claim": claim.get("claim"),
                    "claim_type": claim_type,
                    "evidence_ids": claim.get("evidence_ids", []),
                    "limitations": [
                        "Provider claim is not independent measurement.",
                        "Capability does not establish reliability or safety.",
                    ],
                }
            )

        measured: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for br in results_by_model.get(m.model_id, []):
            if br.source_type in INDEPENDENT_EVAL_SOURCES:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.INDEPENDENTLY_MEASURED
            elif br.source_type in INTERNAL_EVAL_SOURCES:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.AUTHORIZED_INTERNAL_MEASUREMENT
            elif br.source_type in PROVIDER_CLAIM_SOURCES:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED
            else:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.UNKNOWN

            measured.append(
                {
                    "result_id": br.result_id,
                    "benchmark_id": br.benchmark_id,
                    "benchmark_version": br.benchmark_version,
                    "score": br.score,
                    "metric": br.metric,
                    "claim_type": ctype.name,
                    "comparability_status": br.comparability_status.name,
                    "evidence_ids": br.evidence_ids,
                    "limitations": br.limitations,
                }
            )

        out.append(
            {
                "model_id": m.model_id,
                "canonical_name": m.canonical_name,
                "claims": claims,
                "measurements": measured,
                "assessment": "CLAIMS_AND_MEASUREMENTS_SEPARATED",
                "limitations": [
                    "Capability is not reliability.",
                    "Capability is not safety.",
                    "Benchmark performance is task/distribution/protocol specific.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def normalize_benchmark_results(
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    groups: Dict[str, List[BenchmarkResult]] = defaultdict(list)
    conflicts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for br in benchmark_results:
        if br.protocol_complete:
            group_key = stable_id(
                "BMGROUP",
                br.benchmark_id,
                br.benchmark_version,
                br.metric,
                br.prompting,
                br.shots,
                tuple(br.tools or []),
                br.sampling,
                br.evaluator,
            )
            br.comparability_group = group_key
            br.comparability_status = BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABLE
            groups[group_key].append(br)
        else:
            group_key = f"LIMITED::{br.result_id}"
            br.comparability_group = group_key
            br.comparability_status = BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED
            groups[group_key].append(br)

    group_objects: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    for gid, items in groups.items():
        scores = [x.score for x in items if x.score is not None]
        conflict = False

        if len(scores) > 1:
            mean = statistics.mean(scores)
            tolerance = max(1.0, abs(mean) * 0.02)
            if max(scores) - min(scores) > tolerance:
                conflict = True
                for x in items:
                    x.comparability_status = BenchmarkComparability.NOT_DIRECTLY_COMPARABLE
                conflicts.append(
                    {
                        "group_id": gid,
                        "result_ids": [x.result_id for x in items],
                        "scores": scores,
                        "description": "Same protocol group produced materially different scores.",
                    }
                )

        group_objects[gid] = {
            "group_id": gid,
            "result_ids": [x.result_id for x in items],
            "protocol_complete": bool(items and items[0].protocol_complete),
            "conflict": conflict,
            "comparability_status": items[0].comparability_status.name if items else BenchmarkComparability.UNKNOWN.name,
        }

    return group_objects, conflicts


def get_related_models(
    entity_id: Optional[str],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    depth: int = 2,
) -> Set[str]:
    if not entity_id:
        return set()

    relations = {
        "USES_MODEL",
        "PRIMARY_MODEL_OF",
        "SERVES_MODEL",
        "DEPLOYS_MODEL",
        "ROUTES_TO",
        "USES_API",
        "BASED_ON",
        "VERSION_OF",
    }

    visited: Set[str] = set()
    frontier: Set[str] = {entity_id}
    models: Set[str] = set()

    for _ in range(depth):
        next_frontier: Set[str] = set()

        for node in frontier:
            if node in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(node)

            ent = entity_index.get(node)
            if ent and ent.entity_type == "MODEL":
                models.add(node)

            for r in rel_by_source.get(node, []):
                if r.relation in relations:
                    next_frontier.add(r.target_id)

            for r in rel_by_target.get(node, []):
                if r.relation in relations:
                    next_frontier.add(r.source_id)

        frontier = next_frontier - visited

    return models


def map_security_layers(
    findings: List[SecurityFinding],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for f in findings:
        affected_id = f.affected_entity_id
        affected_type = (f.affected_entity_type or "unknown").upper()
        related_models = get_related_models(affected_id, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index, depth=2)

        if affected_type in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"}:
            layer = "MODEL"
            base_model_impact = "SUPPORTED" if f.verification_state == VerificationState.SUPPORTED else "PARTIAL"
        elif affected_type in {"PRODUCT", "APPLICATION", "API", "DEPLOYMENT", "AGENT"}:
            layer = affected_type
            if f.attributes.get("base_model_vulnerable") is True:
                base_model_impact = "CANDIDATE_REQUIRES_VALIDATION"
            else:
                base_model_impact = "NOT_ESTABLISHED"
        else:
            layer = affected_type or "UNKNOWN"
            base_model_impact = "UNKNOWN"

        out.append(
            {
                "finding_id": f.finding_id,
                "affected_layer": layer,
                "affected_entity_id": affected_id,
                "affected_version": f.affected_version,
                "related_model_ids": sorted(related_models),
                "base_model_impact": base_model_impact,
                "issue_type": f.issue_type.name,
                "mitigation": f.mitigation,
                "fixed_version": f.fixed_version,
                "evidence_ids": f.evidence_ids,
                "limitations": f.limitations,
            }
        )

    return out


def propagate_incidents(
    incidents: List[Incident],
    entities: List[Entity],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    deployment_like_types = {"DEPLOYMENT", "APPLICATION", "PRODUCT", "AGENT"}

    for inc in incidents:
        model_id = inc.model_id
        if not model_id and inc.system_id and entity_index.get(inc.system_id) and entity_index[inc.system_id].entity_type == "MODEL":
            model_id = inc.system_id

        affected_versions = set(inc.affected_versions or [])
        affected: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for ent in entities:
            if ent.entity_type not in deployment_like_types:
                continue

            related_models = get_related_models(ent.entity_id, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index, depth=2)

            if model_id and model_id not in related_models:
                continue

            version = ent.attributes.get("model_version") or ent.attributes.get("version")
            if isinstance(version, list):
                version = version[0] if version else None

            if affected_versions and version and str(version) in affected_versions:
                state = "DEPLOYED_DURING_WINDOW"
            elif not affected_versions:
                state = "POTENTIALLY_AFFECTED"
            elif version and str(version) not in affected_versions:
                state = "NOT_AFFECTED_CANDIDATE"
            else:
                state = "POTENTIALLY_AFFECTED"

            affected.append(
                {
                    "entity_id": ent.entity_id,
                    "entity_type": ent.entity_type,
                    "state": state,
                    "observed_version": version,
                    "evidence_ids": ent.evidence_ids + inc.evidence_ids,
                }
            )

        out.append(
            {
                "incident_id": inc.incident_id,
                "model_id": model_id,
                "affected_versions": sorted(affected_versions),
                "deployment_propagation": affected if affected else [{"state": "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE", "entity_id": None}],
                "limitations": [
                    "Incident propagation is version/deployment specific.",
                    "No deployment evidence does not prove no impact; it may reflect missing inventory.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def map_supply_chain(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    entities: List[Entity],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    supply_relations = {
        "DEPENDS_ON",
        "RUNS_ON",
        "SERVED_BY",
        "HOSTED_BY",
        "USES_FRAMEWORK",
        "USES_PACKAGE",
        "USES_INFERENCE_ENGINE",
        "USES_CLOUD",
        "RUNS_ON_CLOUD",
        "RUNS_ON_HARDWARE",
        "RETRIEVES_FROM",
        "EMBEDDED_BY",
        "RERANKED_BY",
        "USES_VECTOR_DATABASE",
        "GUARDED_BY",
        "MONITORED_BY",
        "USES_PROVIDER",
        "PROVIDED_BY",
    }

    paths: List[List[str]] = []

    for m in models:
        visited: Set[str] = set()
        frontier: List[List[str]] = [[m.model_id]]

        while frontier:
            path = frontier.pop(0)
            node = path[-1]

            if node in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(node)

            if len(path) > 1:
                paths.append(path)

            if len(path) >= 6:
                continue

            for r in rel_by_source.get(node, []):
                if r.relation in supply_relations or r.relation.startswith(("USES_", "RUNS_ON_", "DEPENDS_ON_")):
                    frontier.append(path + [r.target_id])

    dep_type_map = {
        "USES_PROVIDER": "PROVIDER",
        "PROVIDED_BY": "PROVIDER",
        "HOSTED_BY": "PROVIDER",
        "USES_FRAMEWORK": "FRAMEWORK",
        "USES_PACKAGE": "PACKAGE",
        "DEPENDS_ON_PACKAGE": "PACKAGE",
        "USES_INFERENCE_ENGINE": "INFERENCE_ENGINE",
        "RUNS_ON_HARDWARE": "HARDWARE",
        "USES_CLOUD": "CLOUD",
        "RUNS_ON_CLOUD": "CLOUD",
        "RETRIEVES_FROM": "RAG",
        "EMBEDDED_BY": "EMBEDDING_MODEL",
        "RERANKED_BY": "RERANKER",
        "USES_VECTOR_DATABASE": "VECTOR_DATABASE",
        "GUARDED_BY": "GUARDRAIL",
        "MONITORED_BY": "MONITORING",
    }

    deployment_deps: Dict[str, Set[Tuple[str, str]]] = defaultdict(set)
    dep_to_deployments: Dict[Tuple[str, str], Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)

    for ent in entities:
        if ent.entity_type not in {"DEPLOYMENT", "APPLICATION", "PRODUCT", "AGENT"}:
            continue

        for r in rel_by_source.get(ent.entity_id, []):
            dep_type = dep_type_map.get(r.relation)
            if dep_type:
                deployment_deps[ent.entity_id].add((dep_type, r.target_id))
                dep_to_deployments[(dep_type, r.target_id)].add(ent.entity_id)

        related_models = get_related_models(ent.entity_id, rel_by_source, defaultdict(list), entity_index, depth=2)
        for mid in related_models:
            deployment_deps[ent.entity_id].add(("MODEL", mid))
            dep_to_deployments[("MODEL", mid)].add(ent.entity_id)

    common_mode = [
        {
            "dependency_type": dtype,
            "dependency_id": did,
            "deployment_ids": sorted(deps),
            "risk_note": "Shared dependency may create common-mode failure or correlated change risk.",
        }
        for (dtype, did), deps in dep_to_deployments.items()
        if len(deps) > 1
    ]

    return {
        "paths": paths[:200],
        "common_mode_dependencies": common_mode,
        "limitations": [
            "Supply-chain map is evidence-based and may be incomplete.",
            "Common-mode dependency is resilience context, not attack targeting.",
        ],
    }


def estimate_costs(
    pricing: List[Pricing],
    usage_items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    now: datetime,
) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    missing: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    pricing_by_entity: Dict[str, List[Pricing]] = defaultdict(list)
    for p in pricing:
        if p.entity_id:
            pricing_by_entity[p.entity_id].append(p)

    def select_pricing(entity_id: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Pricing]:
        if not entity_id:
            return None
        candidates = pricing_by_entity.get(entity_id, [])
        if not candidates:
            return None

        active = [
            p
            for p in candidates
            if (p.effective_date is None or p.effective_date <= now)
            and (p.expiration_date is None or p.expiration_date >= now)
        ]

        pool = active or candidates
        return max(pool, key=lambda x: x.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc))

    def find_entity_for_usage(usage: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[str]:
        for key in ("pricing_entity_id", "api_id", "model_id", "provider_id", "deployment_id"):
            val = usage.get(key)
            if val:
                return str(val)
        return None

    for usage in usage_items or []:
        entity_id = find_entity_for_usage(usage)
        p = select_pricing(entity_id)

        input_tokens = to_float(usage.get("input_tokens")) or 0.0
        output_tokens = to_float(usage.get("output_tokens")) or 0.0
        cached_tokens = to_float(usage.get("cached_tokens")) or 0.0
        period_days = to_float(usage.get("period_days")) or 30.0

        if not p:
            missing.append(
                {
                    "usage_id": usage.get("usage_id") or new_id("USAGE"),
                    "entity_id": entity_id,
                    "reason": "No applicable pricing record found.",
                    "evidence_ids": usage.get("evidence_ids", []),
                }
            )
            continue

        input_cost = (input_tokens / 1_000_000.0) * (p.input_token_price_per_million or 0.0)
        output_cost = (output_tokens / 1_000_000.0) * (p.output_token_price_per_million or 0.0)
        cached_cost = (cached_tokens / 1_000_000.0) * (p.cached_token_price_per_million or 0.0)
        total = input_cost + output_cost + cached_cost

        estimates.append(
            {
                "estimate_id": new_id("COST"),
                "entity_id": p.entity_id,
                "pricing_id": p.pricing_id,
                "currency": p.currency,
                "input_tokens": input_tokens,
                "output_tokens": output_tokens,
                "cached_tokens": cached_tokens,
                "period_days": period_days,
                "input_cost": round(input_cost, 6),
                "output_cost": round(output_cost, 6),
                "cached_cost": round(cached_cost, 6),
                "total_cost": round(total, 6),
                "formula": "(input_tokens/1e6 * input_price_per_m) + (output_tokens/1e6 * output_price_per_m) + (cached_tokens/1e6 * cached_price_per_m)",
                "pricing_effective_date": p.effective_date,
                "limitations": [
                    "Deterministic arithmetic only.",
                    "Token price is not total infrastructure or engineering cost.",
                    "Pricing is time-specific.",
                ],
                "evidence_ids": sorted(set(usage.get("evidence_ids", []) + p.evidence_ids)),
            }
        )

    return estimates, missing


def assess_substitutability(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    models_by_id = {m.model_id: m for m in models}

    primary_ids = [
        rr["primary_id"]
        for rr in resolved_references
        if rr.get("primary_id") and rr.get("layer") == "MODEL"
    ]

    if not primary_ids and models:
        primary_ids = [models[0].model_id]

    for pid in primary_ids:
        primary = models_by_id.get(pid)
        if not primary:
            continue

        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for m in models:
            if m.model_id == pid:
                continue

            reasons: List[str] = []
            state = Substitutability.UNKNOWN

            same_family = bool(primary.family_id and primary.family_id == m.family_id)
            same_access = primary.access_type == m.access_type and primary.access_type != AccessType.UNKNOWN
            context_ok = (
                primary.context_window is not None
                and m.context_window is not None
                and m.context_window >= primary.context_window
            )
            interfaces_primary = set(primary.attributes.get("supported_interfaces", []) or [])
            interfaces_candidate = set(m.attributes.get("supported_interfaces", []) or [])
            interface_overlap = bool(interfaces_primary & interfaces_candidate)

            tool_primary = bool(primary.attributes.get("tool_use") or primary.attributes.get("function_calling"))
            tool_candidate = bool(m.attributes.get("tool_use") or m.attributes.get("function_calling"))

            if same_family and same_access and context_ok and (interface_overlap or tool_primary == tool_candidate):
                state = Substitutability.EASY
                reasons.append("Same family/access/context/tool profile.")
            elif primary.developer_id and primary.developer_id == m.developer_id:
                state = Substitutability.MODERATE
                reasons.append("Same developer but version/lineage/capability differences require evaluation.")
            elif set(primary.provider_ids) & set(m.provider_ids):
                state = Substitutability.MODERATE
                reasons.append("Same provider but model/version differences require evaluation.")
            else:
                state = Substitutability.DIFFICULT
                reasons.append("Different family/provider/access profile.")

            if not context_ok:
                reasons.append("Context-window equivalence unresolved.")
            if tool_primary != tool_candidate:
                reasons.append("Tool/function-calling profile differs.")

            candidates.append(
                {
                    "candidate_model_id": m.model_id,
                    "candidate_name": m.canonical_name,
                    "state": state.name,
                    "reasons": reasons,
                    "limitations": [
                        "Substitutability is not capability equivalence.",
                        "Migration requires task-specific evaluation, license review, and deployment testing.",
                    ],
                }
            )

        out.append(
            {
                "primary_model_id": pid,
                "primary_name": primary.canonical_name,
                "candidates": candidates,
            }
        )

    return out


def assess_lock_in(
    entities: List[Entity],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for ent in entities:
        if ent.entity_type not in {"DEPLOYMENT", "APPLICATION", "PRODUCT", "AGENT"}:
            continue

        factors: List[str] = []

        for r in rel_by_source.get(ent.entity_id, []):
            target = entity_index.get(r.target_id)
            if not target:
                continue

            if r.relation == "USES_API" and target.attributes.get("proprietary") is True:
                factors.append("Proprietary API dependency.")
            if r.relation in {"EMBEDDED_BY", "USES_VECTOR_DATABASE"}:
                factors.append("Embedding/vector-store coupling.")
            if r.relation == "USES_MODEL":
                model = entity_index.get(r.target_id)
                if model and model.attributes.get("fine_tuned") is True:
                    factors.append("Fine-tuned model coupling.")

        if ent.attributes.get("system_prompt_reference"):
            factors.append("System prompt coupling.")
        if ent.attributes.get("tool_schema_lock_in"):
            factors.append("Tool schema coupling.")
        if ent.attributes.get("stored_conversations") or ent.attributes.get("stored_embeddings"):
            factors.append("Stored data coupling.")

        count = len(set(factors))
        if count == 0:
            state = "LOW"
        elif count <= 2:
            state = "MODERATE"
        else:
            state = "HIGH"

        out.append(
            {
                "entity_id": ent.entity_id,
                "entity_type": ent.entity_type,
                "state": state,
                "factors": sorted(set(factors)),
                "limitations": [
                    "Lock-in is architectural/operational context, not inherently bad.",
                    "Migration difficulty requires substitutability and evaluation evidence.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def analyze_changes(change_events: List[ChangeEvent]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    ordered = sorted(
        change_events,
        key=lambda x: x.effective_at or x.announced_at or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    flags: List[str] = []
    timeline: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for ch in ordered:
        timeline.append(
            {
                "change_id": ch.change_id,
                "entity_id": ch.entity_id,
                "change_type": ch.change_type.name,
                "effective_at": ch.effective_at,
                "announced_at": ch.announced_at,
                "old_state": ch.old_state,
                "new_state": ch.new_state,
                "impact_candidate": ch.impact_candidate,
                "evidence_ids": ch.evidence_ids,
                "limitations": ch.limitations,
            }
        )

        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.ALIAS_ROUTE_CHANGE:
            flags.append("ALIAS_ROUTE_CHANGE_REPRODUCIBILITY_RISK")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.MODEL_SWITCH:
            flags.append("MODEL_SWITCH_REQUIRES_EVALUATION")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.PRICING_CHANGE:
            flags.append("PRICING_CHANGE_TIME_BOUND_COST_CONTEXT")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.LICENSE_CHANGE:
            flags.append("LICENSE_CHANGE_REQUIRES_LEGAL_REVIEW")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.GUARDRAIL_UPDATE:
            flags.append("GUARDRAIL_UPDATE_MAY_SHIFT_SAFETY_BEHAVIOR")

    return {
        "timeline": timeline,
        "flags": sorted(set(flags)),
        "limitations": [
            "Change is not automatically improvement.",
            "Behavior drift may arise from model, prompt, RAG, tool, guardrail, provider, or routing changes.",
        ],
    }


def analyze_source_independence(evidences: List[EvidenceRef]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    upstream_groups: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    source_groups: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)

    for ev in evidences:
        upstream_groups[ev.upstream_source_id].append(ev.evidence_id)
        source_groups[ev.source_id].append(ev.evidence_id)

    dependent_roots = {root for root, ids in upstream_groups.items() if len(ids) > 1}

    if not upstream_groups:
        state = SourceIndependenceState.UNKNOWN
    elif not dependent_roots:
        state = SourceIndependenceState.INDEPENDENT
    elif len(dependent_roots) < len(upstream_groups):
        state = SourceIndependenceState.PARTIALLY_DEPENDENT
    else:
        state = SourceIndependenceState.DEPENDENT

    return {
        "state": state.name,
        "total_evidence_items": len(evidences),
        "unique_sources": len(source_groups),
        "unique_upstream_families": len(upstream_groups),
        "dependent_upstream_families": sorted(dependent_roots),
        "upstream_groups": {root: sorted(ids) for root, ids in upstream_groups.items()},
        "limitations": [
            "Multiple articles quoting one model card are not independent sources.",
            "Multiple leaderboards using the same evaluation harness/data are not independent evidence.",
        ],
    }


def detect_contradictions(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    alias_to_ids: Dict[str, Set[str]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_conflicts: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    pricing: List[Pricing],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_events: List[ChangeEvent],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
) -> List[Contradiction]:
    out: List[Contradiction] = []

    for rr in resolved_references:
        if rr.get("ambiguous"):
            out.append(
                Contradiction(
                    contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                    contradiction_type="REFERENCE_AMBIGUITY",
                    description=f"Reference '{rr.get('reference')}' resolved to multiple entities: {rr.get('resolved_ids')}.",
                    object_ids=rr.get("resolved_ids", []),
                    evidence_ids=rr.get("evidence_ids", []),
                    severity="MEDIUM",
                )
            )

    for alias, ids in alias_to_ids.items():
        model_ids = [iid for iid in ids if entity_index.get(iid) and entity_index[iid].entity_type == "MODEL"]
        if len(set(model_ids)) > 1:
            out.append(
                Contradiction(
                    contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                    contradiction_type="MODEL_ALIAS_AMBIGUITY",
                    description=f"Alias '{alias}' maps to multiple model identities: {sorted(set(model_ids))}.",
                    object_ids=sorted(set(model_ids)),
                    evidence_ids=[],
                    severity="HIGH",
                )
            )

    for conflict in benchmark_conflicts:
        out.append(
            Contradiction(
                contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                contradiction_type="BENCHMARK_SCORE_CONFLICT",
                description=conflict.get("description", "Benchmark protocol group produced conflicting scores."),
                object_ids=conflict.get("result_ids", []),
                evidence_ids=[],
                severity="MEDIUM",
            )
        )

    pricing_by_entity: Dict[str, List[Pricing]] = defaultdict(list)
    for p in pricing:
        if p.entity_id:
            pricing_by_entity[p.entity_id].append(p)

    for entity_id, items in pricing_by_entity.items():
        sorted_items = sorted(items, key=lambda x: x.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc))
        for i in range(len(sorted_items)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(sorted_items)):
                a = sorted_items[i]
                b = sorted_items[j]
                a_start = a.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                a_end = a.expiration_date or (a_start + timedelta(days=3650))
                b_start = b.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                b_end = b.expiration_date or (b_start + timedelta(days=3650))

                if a_start <= b_end and b_start <= a_end:
                    if (
                        a.input_token_price_per_million is not None
                        and b.input_token_price_per_million is not None
                        and abs(a.input_token_price_per_million - b.input_token_price_per_million) > 1e-9
                    ):
                        out.append(
                            Contradiction(
                                contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                                contradiction_type="PRICING_OVERLAP_CONFLICT",
                                description=f"Overlapping pricing records for {entity_id} disagree on input token price.",
                                object_ids=[a.pricing_id, b.pricing_id],
                                evidence_ids=a.evidence_ids + b.evidence_ids,
                                severity="MEDIUM",
                            )
                        )

    for sm in security_mappings:
        if sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} and sm.get("base_model_impact") == "CANDIDATE_REQUIRES_VALIDATION":
            out.append(
                Contradiction(
                    contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                    contradiction_type="SECURITY_LAYER_CONFUSION_CANDIDATE",
                    description=f"Finding {sm.get('finding_id')} affects {sm.get('affected_layer')} but claims base-model vulnerability.",
                    object_ids=[sm.get("finding_id") or "", sm.get("affected_entity_id") or ""],
                    evidence_ids=[],
                    severity="HIGH",
                )
            )

    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in relationships:
        rel_by_source[r.source_id].append(r)

    for ch in change_events:
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.MODEL_SWITCH and ch.entity_id and ch.effective_at:
            old_model = None
            new_model = None

            if isinstance(ch.old_state, dict):
                old_model = ch.old_state.get("model_id") or ch.old_state.get("value")
            else:
                old_model = ch.old_state

            if isinstance(ch.new_state, dict):
                new_model = ch.new_state.get("model_id") or ch.new_state.get("value")
            else:
                new_model = ch.new_state

            if old_model:
                for r in rel_by_source.get(ch.entity_id, []):
                    if r.relation == "USES_MODEL" and r.target_id == old_model and (r.valid_to is None or r.valid_to > ch.effective_at):
                        out.append(
                            Contradiction(
                                contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                                contradiction_type="DEPLOYMENT_STALE_MODEL_RELATIONSHIP_CANDIDATE",
                                description=f"Change event indicates model switch for {ch.entity_id}, but relationship to old model {old_model} remains active after effective time.",
                                object_ids=[ch.entity_id, old_model, r.relationship_id],
                                evidence_ids=ch.evidence_ids + r.evidence_ids,
                                severity="MEDIUM",
                            )
                        )

    return out


def build_hypotheses(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    known_facts: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[Hypothesis]:
    hypotheses: List[Hypothesis] = []

    alias_ambiguous = any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references)
    exact_version_resolved = any(rr.get("layer") == "MODEL" and not rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references)
    benchmark_limited = any(g.get("comparability_status") == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED.name for g in benchmark_groups.values())
    benchmark_conflict = any(g.get("conflict") for g in benchmark_groups.values())
    product_security = any(sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} for sm in security_mappings)
    incident_no_deployment = any(
        any(p.get("state") == "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE" for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    )
    common_mode = bool(supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"))
    alias_change = "ALIAS_ROUTE_CHANGE_REPRODUCIBILITY_RISK" in change_analysis.get("flags", [])

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H1",
            description="The referenced AI entity is resolved to an exact model/version/checkpoint.",
            support_evidence=["Non-ambiguous model reference resolved." if exact_version_resolved else ""],
            opposition_evidence=["Alias/reference ambiguity detected." if alias_ambiguous else ""],
            unknowns=["Exact checkpoint behind mutable API alias.", "Regional/provider routing variants."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if alias maps to multiple snapshots or provider routing is undisclosed.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.CANDIDATE if exact_version_resolved and not alias_ambiguous else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H2",
            description="Observed product/application capability is attributable to the base model alone.",
            support_evidence=["No RAG/tool/guardrail dependencies observed."] if not common_mode else [],
            opposition_evidence=["Product/deployment relationships include retrieval/tools/guardrails."] if common_mode or product_security else [],
            unknowns=["System prompt", "Tool permissions", "RAG corpus version", "Guardrail effectiveness."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if capability depends on retrieval, tools, prompts, or policy layers.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.REJECTED if common_mode or product_security else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H3",
            description="Benchmark scores are directly comparable across reported systems.",
            support_evidence=["Complete protocol groups without conflict."] if not benchmark_limited and not benchmark_conflict else [],
            opposition_evidence=["Protocol-limited groups."] if benchmark_limited else [],
            unknowns=["Prompting", "shots", "tools", "sampling", "evaluator", "benchmark version."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if protocol fields differ or scores conflict under same protocol.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.CANDIDATE if not benchmark_limited and not benchmark_conflict else HypothesisStatus.REJECTED,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H4",
            description="Security finding affects the base model rather than product/API/deployment layer.",
            support_evidence=[],
            opposition_evidence=["Finding mapped to non-model layer."] if product_security else [],
            unknowns=["Model version", "deployment configuration", "guardrail layer", "tool authorization."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if advisory evidence ties issue to retrieval, prompt, tool, or runtime policy layer.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H5",
            description="Incident impacted all deployments using the referenced model.",
            support_evidence=[],
            opposition_evidence=["No deployment evidence or version mismatch."] if incident_no_deployment else [],
            unknowns=["Exact deployed versions", "time windows", "regional routing."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if only specific versions/deployments were affected or inventory is missing.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H6",
            description="Multiple systems share common-mode AI dependencies creating correlated risk.",
            support_evidence=["Shared provider/model/framework/vector-store/guardrail dependencies observed."] if common_mode else [],
            opposition_evidence=[],
            unknowns=["Hidden routing", "fallback models", "embedding migration constraints."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if dependencies are independent across version/provider/infrastructure.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.ACTIVE if common_mode else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H7",
            description="API alias route change may cause behavior or cost drift.",
            support_evidence=["Alias route change event observed."] if alias_change else [],
            opposition_evidence=["No alias change event."] if not alias_change else [],
            unknowns=["Routing policy", "snapshot equivalence", "evaluation delta."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if alias route remained stable or new snapshot is proven equivalent.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.ACTIVE if alias_change else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    return [h for h in hypotheses if h.support_evidence or h.opposition_evidence or h.unknowns]


def skeptic_review(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    known_facts: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    flags: List[str] = []
    alternatives: List[str] = []
    questions: List[str] = []

    if any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references):
        flags.append("ENTITY_RESOLUTION_AMBIGUOUS")
        alternatives.append("Reference may denote product, API alias, deployment, or model family rather than exact checkpoint.")

    if any(g.get("comparability_status") == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED.name for g in benchmark_groups.values()):
        flags.append("BENCHMARK_COMPARABILITY_LIMITED")
        alternatives.append("Scores may use different prompting, shots, tools, sampling, evaluator, or benchmark version.")

    if any(g.get("conflict") for g in benchmark_groups.values()):
        flags.append("BENCHMARK_SCORE_CONFLICT")
        alternatives.append("Conflict may reflect non-independent republication, protocol drift, or measurement error.")

    if any(sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} for sm in security_mappings):
        flags.append("SECURITY_LAYER_NOT_MODEL")
        alternatives.append("Issue may reside in RAG, prompt, tool authorization, output handling, or runtime policy.")

    if any(
        any(p.get("state") == "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE" for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    ):
        flags.append("INCIDENT_DEPLOYMENT_SCOPE_UNRESOLVED")
        alternatives.append("Missing deployment inventory may hide affected systems.")

    if supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        flags.append("COMMON_MODE_DEPENDENCY")
        alternatives.append("Shared dependency may be intentional, contracted, or an artifact of incomplete inventory.")

    if change_analysis.get("flags"):
        flags.extend(change_analysis["flags"])
        alternatives.append("Behavior change may be operational rollout rather than degradation.")

    if contradictions:
        flags.append("CONTRADICTIONS_PRESENT")
        alternatives.append("Contradictions may reflect stale docs, alias routing, provider updates, or source dependency.")

    questions.extend(
        [
            "Did we separate model, product, API, deployment, and agent?",
            "Is the exact version/checkpoint resolved or only an alias?",
            "Are benchmark protocols normalized?",
            "Is capability provider-claimed or independently measured?",
            "Could RAG/tools/guardrails explain observed behavior?",
            "Does security finding affect base model or surrounding system?",
            "Are multiple sources actually independent?",
            "What evidence would disprove supply-chain or incident scope claims?",
        ]
    )

    return {
        "flags": sorted(set(flags)),
        "alternative_explanations": sorted(set(alternatives)),
        "diagnostic_questions": questions,
        "review_outcome": "PARTIAL_AGREEMENT" if flags else "AGREE",
        "boundary": "Defensive intelligence only. No model theft, prompt extraction, safeguard bypass, poisoning, or harmful agent enablement.",
    }


def build_facts(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    source_independence: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[FactRecord]:
    facts: List[FactRecord] = []

    for rr in resolved_references:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"AI reference '{rr.get('reference')}' resolved to layer {rr.get('layer')} with primary id {rr.get('primary_id')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.INDEPENDENT_OBSERVATION.name if not rr.get("ambiguous") else ClaimType.HYPOTHESIS.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.SUPPORTED if not rr.get("ambiguous") else VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE,
                evidence_ids=rr.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=rr.get("resolved_ids", []),
                confidence=0.8 if not rr.get("ambiguous") else 0.45,
                limitations=["Reference resolution may be alias-dependent."],
            )
        )

    for m in models:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Model record observed: {m.canonical_name} ({m.model_id}), type={m.model_type.name}, access={m.access_type.name}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.OFFICIAL_CLAIM.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=m.evidence_ids,
                object_ids=[m.model_id],
                confidence=m.confidence,
                limitations=m.limitations,
            )
        )

    for lin in lineage:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Model lineage edge observed: {lin.get('source_model_id')} {lin.get('relation')} {lin.get('target_model_id')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.DERIVED_RESULT.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=lin.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=[lin.get("source_model_id") or "", lin.get("target_model_id") or ""],
                confidence=float(lin.get("confidence", 0.5)),
                limitations=lin.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    for br in benchmark_results:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Benchmark result observed: model={br.model_id}, benchmark={br.benchmark_id}/{br.benchmark_version}, score={br.score}, comparability={br.comparability_status.name}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.MEASURED_RESULT.name if br.source_type in INDEPENDENT_EVAL_SOURCES else ClaimType.OFFICIAL_CLAIM.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=br.evidence_ids,
                object_ids=[br.result_id],
                confidence=br.confidence,
                limitations=br.limitations,
            )
        )

    for sm in security_mappings:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Security finding mapped to layer {sm.get('affected_layer')}; base-model impact={sm.get('base_model_impact')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.INDEPENDENT_OBSERVATION.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=[],
                object_ids=[sm.get("finding_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.7,
                limitations=["Layer mapping does not establish exploitation."],
            )
        )

    for inc in incident_props:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Incident {inc.get('incident_id')} propagation state resolved against available deployment inventory.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.ANALYST_INFERENCE.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE,
                evidence_ids=[],
                object_ids=[inc.get("incident_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.6,
                limitations=inc.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    if supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement="Common-mode AI dependencies observed across multiple deployments/applications.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.ANALYST_INFERENCE.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=[],
                object_ids=[],
                confidence=0.7,
                limitations=supply_chain.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    for est in cost_estimates:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Deterministic cost estimate computed for entity {est.get('entity_id')} using pricing {est.get('pricing_id')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.DERIVED_RESULT.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=est.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=[est.get("estimate_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.9,
                limitations=est.get("limitations", []),
            )
        )

    for ch in change_analysis.get("timeline", []):
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Change event observed: {ch.get('change_type')} for entity {ch.get('entity_id')} effective {ch.get('effective_at')}.",
                claim_type=ClaimType.OFFICIAL_CLAIM.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
                evidence_ids=ch.get("evidence_ids", []),
                object_ids=[ch.get("change_id") or ""],
                confidence=0.75,
                limitations=["Change does not automatically imply improvement or degradation."],
            )
        )

    for c in contradictions:
        facts.append(
            FactRecord(
                fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
                statement=f"Contradiction: {c.contradiction_type} — {c.description}",
                claim_type=ClaimType.UNKNOWN.name,
                verification_state=VerificationState.DISPUTED,
                evidence_ids=c.evidence_ids,
                object_ids=c.object_ids,
                confidence=0.75,
                limitations=["Contradictions must be preserved, not silently resolved."],
            )
        )

    facts.append(
        FactRecord(
            fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
            statement=f"Source independence state: {source_independence.get('state')}.",
            claim_type=ClaimType.ANALYST_INFERENCE.name,
            verification_state=VerificationState.PARTIALLY_SUPPORTED,
            evidence_ids=[],
            object_ids=[],
            confidence=0.7,
            limitations=source_independence.get("limitations", []),
        )
    )

    facts.append(
        FactRecord(
            fact_id=new_id("FACT"),
            statement="No autonomous capability, harmful intent, model theft, safeguard bypass, or real-world attribution is established by AIINT artifact intelligence alone.",
            claim_type=ClaimType.UNKNOWN.name,
            verification_state=VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE,
            evidence_ids=[],
            object_ids=[],
            confidence=0.99,
            limitations=["Consequential security, legal, governance, and attribution decisions require human review."],
        )
    )

    return facts


def build_gaps(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    cost_missing: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
) -> List[Gap]:
    gaps: List[Gap] = []

    def add_gap(description: str, importance: str, recommended_source: str, specialist: str, expected_information_value: str) -> None:
        gaps.append(
            Gap(
                gap_id=new_id("GAP"),
                description=description,
                importance=importance,
                recommended_source=recommended_source,
                specialist=specialist,
                expected_information_value=expected_information_value,
            )
        )

    if not resolved_references or any(not rr.get("primary_id") for rr in resolved_references):
        add_gap(
            "AI entity reference unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "official model card / provider API docs / authorized model inventory",
            "AIINT",
            "Establishes whether reference denotes model, API, product, deployment, or agent.",
        )

    if any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references):
        add_gap(
            "Model/API/product alias ambiguity detected.",
            "HIGH",
            "release notes / versioned endpoint metadata / deployment logs",
            "AIINT / CLOUDINT",
            "Resolves exact version/checkpoint and avoids layer confusion.",
        )

    if not any(m.model_id for m in models if m.access_type != AccessType.UNKNOWN):
        add_gap(
            "Model access type unknown.",
            "MEDIUM",
            "license text / provider docs / model repository",
            "AIINT / LEGALINT",
            "Distinguishes open weights, API-only, hosted service, and proprietary access.",
        )

    if not lineage:
        add_gap(
            "Model lineage unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "official model card / technical report / release notes",
            "AIINT",
            "Separates base, fine-tune, adapter, quantized, and distilled variants.",
        )

    if any(br.comparability_status == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED for br in benchmark_results):
        add_gap(
            "Benchmark protocol incomplete for one or more results.",
            "HIGH",
            "evaluation harness / benchmark card / reproducible submission",
            "AIINT / EVALINT",
            "Enables apples-to-apples comparison and avoids leaderboard overclaim.",
        )

    if any(sm.get("base_model_impact") in {"NOT_ESTABLISHED", "CANDIDATE_REQUIRES_VALIDATION"} for sm in security_mappings):
        add_gap(
            "Security finding layer mapping to base model unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "advisory details / affected component metadata / deployment version",
            "AIINT / VULNINT / INCIDENTINT",
            "Prevents product/API/RAG/tool issue from being misattributed to base model.",
        )

    if any(
        any(p.get("state") in {"NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE", "POTENTIALLY_AFFECTED"} for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    ):
        add_gap(
            "Incident deployment scope unresolved.",
            "HIGH",
            "authorized model inventory / deployment logs / provider status",
            "AIINT / CLOUDINT / INCIDENTINT",
            "Determines which applications/deployments used affected version during window.",
        )

    if not supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        add_gap(
            "Common-mode dependency analysis incomplete.",
            "MEDIUM",
            "authorized AI inventory / SBOM / inference stack metadata",
            "AIINT / SUPPLYCHAININT",
            "Identifies shared provider/model/framework/vector-store/guardrail concentration.",
        )

    if cost_missing:
        add_gap(
            "Pricing record missing or not time-aligned for cost usage.",
            "MEDIUM",
            "provider pricing page / contract / cloud billing export",
            "AIINT / FINOPS",
            "Enables deterministic cost estimation with as-of-date context.",
        )

    if change_analysis.get("flags"):
        add_gap(
            "Change/drift event requires evaluation and deployment correlation.",
            "HIGH",
            "release notes / model version logs / evaluation harness",
            "AIINT / INCIDENTINT",
            "Distinguishes intentional update from behavior/cost/safety drift.",
        )

    if contradictions:
        add_gap(
            "Evidence contradictions require source/time/version reconciliation.",
            "HIGH",
            "primary provider docs / versioned artifacts / independent evaluation",
            "AIINT",
            "Prevents alias, stale docs, or dependent sources from driving false conclusions.",
        )

    add_gap(
        "Real-world intent, maliciousness, or actor attribution unresolved.",
        "HIGH",
        "authorized incident/CTI/legal review",
        "CTI / INCIDENTINT / LEGALINT",
        "Prevents technical AI ecosystem intelligence from becoming unsupported attribution.",
    )

    return gaps


def build_graph(
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    facts: List[FactRecord],
    hypotheses: List[Hypothesis],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    gaps: List[Gap],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    nodes: Dict[str, GraphNode] = {}
    edges: List[GraphEdge] = []

    def add_node(node_id: str, node_type: str, attributes: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        nodes[node_id] = GraphNode(node_id=node_id, type=node_type, attributes=attributes)

    def add_edge(source: str, target: str, relation: str, confidence: float, evidence_ids: List[str]) -> None:
        edges.append(
            GraphEdge(
                edge_id=new_id("EDGE"),
                source_node_id=source,
                target_node_id=target,
                relation=relation,
                confidence=confidence,
                evidence_ids=sorted(set(evidence_ids)),
            )
        )

    for e in entity_index.values():
        add_node(
            f"N_ENTITY_{e.entity_id}",
            e.entity_type,
            {
                "name": e.name,
                "aliases": e.aliases,
                "confidence": e.confidence,
                "limitations": e.limitations,
            },
        )

    for r in relationships:
        src = f"N_ENTITY_{r.source_id}"
        tgt = f"N_ENTITY_{r.target_id}"
        if src not in nodes:
            add_node(src, "UNRESOLVED_ENTITY", {"entity_id": r.source_id})
        if tgt not in nodes:
            add_node(tgt, "UNRESOLVED_ENTITY", {"entity_id": r.target_id})
        add_edge(src, tgt, r.relation, r.confidence, r.evidence_ids)

    for lin in lineage:
        src = f"N_ENTITY_{lin.get('source_model_id')}"
        tgt = f"N_ENTITY_{lin.get('target_model_id')}"
        if src not in nodes:
            add_node(src, "MODEL", {"entity_id": lin.get("source_model_id")})
        if tgt not in nodes:
            add_node(tgt, "MODEL", {"entity_id": lin.get("target_model_id")})
        add_edge(src, tgt, str(lin.get("relation")), float(lin.get("confidence", 0.5)), lin.get("evidence_ids", []))

    for br in benchmark_results:
        node_id = f"N_BMRESULT_{br.result_id}"
        add_node(
            node_id,
            "BenchmarkResult",
            {
                "model_id": br.model_id,
                "benchmark_id": br.benchmark_id,
                "score": br.score,
                "comparability_status": br.comparability_status.name,
            },
        )
        if br.model_id:
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{br.model_id}", "SCORED_ON_MODEL", br.confidence, br.evidence_ids)
        if br.benchmark_id:
            if f"N_ENTITY_{br.benchmark_id}" not in nodes:
                add_node(f"N_ENTITY_{br.benchmark_id}", "BENCHMARK", {"benchmark_id": br.benchmark_id})
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{br.benchmark_id}", "USES_BENCHMARK", br.confidence, br.evidence_ids)

    for sm in security_mappings:
        node_id = f"N_SEC_{sm.get('finding_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "SecurityFinding", sm)
        if sm.get("affected_entity_id"):
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{sm.get('affected_entity_id')}", "AFFECTS", 0.7, [])

    for inc in incident_props:
        node_id = f"N_INC_{inc.get('incident_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "Incident", inc)

    for est in cost_estimates:
        node_id = f"N_COST_{est.get('estimate_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "CostEstimate", est)
        if est.get("entity_id"):
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{est.get('entity_id')}", "COST_FOR", 0.9, est.get("evidence_ids", []))

    for ch in change_analysis.get("timeline", []):
        node_id = f"N_CHG_{ch.get('change_id')}"
        add_node(node_id, "ChangeEvent", ch)
        if ch.get("entity_id"):
            add_edge(node_id, f"N_ENTITY_{ch.get('entity_id')}", "CHANGES", 0.8, ch.get("evidence_ids", []))

    for f in facts:
        add_node(f"N_FACT_{f.fact_id}", "Fact", asdict(f))

    for h in hypotheses:
        add_node(f"N_HYP_{h.id}", "Hypothesis", asdict(h))

    for c in contradictions:
        add_node(f"N_CONTRA_{c.contradiction_id}", "Contradiction", asdict(c))

    for g in gaps:
        add_node(f"N_GAP_{g.gap_id}", "Gap", asdict(g))

    return {
        "nodes": [asdict(n) for n in nodes.values()],
        "edges": [asdict(e) for e in edges],
    }


def generate_summary(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
    capability: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    cost_estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    gaps: List[Gap],
    source_independence: Dict[str, Any],
) -> str:
    lines: List[str] = []

    lines.append("=== AIINT REQUIRED ANALYST SUMMARY ===")

    lines.append("AI ENTITY / REFERENCE:")
    for rr in resolved_references:
        lines.append(f"- {rr.get('reference')} -> layer={rr.get('layer')}, primary={rr.get('primary_id')}, ambiguous={rr.get('ambiguous')}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("MODEL / FAMILY / VERSION:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(
            f"- {m.canonical_name} ({m.model_id}): family={m.family_id}, type={m.model_type.name}, access={m.access_type.name}, context={m.context_window}, params={m.parameter_count}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("DEVELOPER / PROVIDER:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(f"- {m.model_id}: developer={m.developer_id}, providers={m.provider_ids}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("PRODUCT / API / DEPLOYMENT / AGENT SEPARATION:")
    lines.append("- References are resolved by layer; product/API/deployment/agent capability is not collapsed into base model capability.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("MODEL LINEAGE:")
    for lin in lineage:
        lines.append(f"- {lin.get('source_model_id')} {lin.get('relation')} {lin.get('target_model_id')} [{lin.get('lineage_confidence')}]")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("MODALITIES / CONTEXT / TOOLS:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(
            f"- {m.model_id}: modality={m.modality}, max_context={m.context_window}, max_output={m.max_output_tokens}, tool_use={m.attributes.get('tool_use')}, function_calling={m.attributes.get('function_calling')}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("CAPABILITY CLAIMS vs MEASUREMENTS:")
    for cap in capability:
        lines.append(f"- {cap.get('model_id')}: claims={len(cap.get('claims', []))}, measurements={len(cap.get('measurements', []))}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("BENCHMARK COMPARABILITY:")
    for br in benchmark_results:
        lines.append(
            f"- {br.result_id}: model={br.model_id}, benchmark={br.benchmark_id}/{br.benchmark_version}, score={br.score}, status={br.comparability_status.name}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("LICENSE / POLICY:")
    for m in models:
        lines.append(f"- {m.model_id}: license={m.license_id}, policies={m.policy_ids}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("TRAINING DATA / DATASET PROVENANCE:")
    lines.append("- Training-data claims are preserved as disclosed/inferred/unknown and are not converted into exact record facts.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("SAFETY / SECURITY:")
    for sm in security_mappings:
        lines.append(
            f"- {sm.get('finding_id')}: layer={sm.get('affected_layer')}, base_model_impact={sm.get('base_model_impact')}, issue={sm.get('issue_type')}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("AI INCIDENTS:")
    for inc in incident_props:
        lines.append(f"- {inc.get('incident_id')}: model={inc.get('model_id')}, propagation_entries={len(inc.get('deployment_propagation', []))}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("RAG / AGENT / TOOL CONTEXT:")
    lines.append("- RAG, embedding, reranker, vector DB, tool, MCP, guardrail, and monitoring dependencies are represented separately from model intrinsic capability.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("FRAMEWORK / PACKAGE / INFERENCE / HARDWARE / CLOUD:")
    if supply_chain.get("paths"):
        for path in supply_chain["paths"][:20]:
            lines.append("- " + " -> ".join(path))
    else:
        lines.append("- No supply-chain paths resolved from supplied relationships.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("COMMON-MODE DEPENDENCIES:")
    for cm in supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies", [])[:20]:
        lines.append(f"- {cm.get('dependency_type')} {cm.get('dependency_id')} shared by {cm.get('deployment_ids')}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("COST / LATENCY / THROUGHPUT:")
    for est in cost_estimates:
        lines.append(
            f"- {est.get('entity_id')}: total_cost={est.get('total_cost')} {est.get('currency')} as_of={est.get('pricing_effective_date')}"
        )

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("CHANGE / DRIFT:")
    for flag in change_analysis.get("flags", []):
        lines.append(f"- {flag}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("SOURCE RELIABILITY / INDEPENDENCE:")
    lines.append(f"- Source independence state: {source_independence.get('state')}")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("CONTRADICTIONS:")
    if contradictions:
        for c in contradictions:
            lines.append(f"- {c.contradiction_type}: {c.description} [{c.severity}]")
    else:
        lines.append("- None detected.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("UNKNOWN:")
    lines.append("- Exact checkpoint behind mutable API alias, if applicable.")
    lines.append("- Effective reasoning quality across full context window.")
    lines.append("- Real-world reliability, safety, and intent.")
    lines.append("- Full deployment scope and hidden routing/fallback behavior.")
    lines.append("- Threat actor, malicious intent, or exploitation status.")

    lines.append("")
    lines.append("NEXT ACTION:")
    lines.append("- Resolve exact model/version/checkpoint and separate API alias routing.")
    lines.append("- Retrieve official model card, system card, release notes, and license text.")
    lines.append("- Normalize benchmark protocol before comparing scores.")
    lines.append("- Map RAG/tool/guardrail/monitoring dependencies before attributing behavior to model.")
    lines.append("- Hand vulnerability applicability to VULNINT and incident scope to INCIDENTINT.")
    lines.append("- Hand package/framework deep analysis to PACKAGEINT and repository details to REPOINT.")
    lines.append("- Hand license/regulatory interpretation to LEGALINT/GRC.")
    lines.append("- Do not extract weights, harvest keys, bypass safeguards, jailbreak, poison, or deploy harmful agents.")

    return "\n".join(lines)


# ==============================================================================
# MAIN ENGINE
# ==============================================================================

class AIIntEmployee:
    """
    Defensive AIINT employee.

    Consumes lawful public/authorized AI ecosystem evidence representations.
    Does not steal models, extract weights, harvest/use API keys, bypass safeguards,
    extract confidential prompts, automate jailbreaks, poison models/data/RAG/memory,
    compromise ML pipelines/inference infrastructure, or deploy harmful autonomous agents.
    """

    def __init__(self, model_mode: str = "LOCAL_ONLY"):
        self.model_mode = model_mode.upper()
        logger.info("AIINT employee initialized in mode=%s", self.model_mode)

    def process_case(
        self,
        case_id: str,
        task_id: str,
        objective: str,
        scope: Dict[str, Any],
        evidence_input: Dict[str, Any],
        known_facts: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        known_facts = known_facts or {}
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        try:
            enforce_policy(objective, scope)
        except PolicyViolation as exc:
            return {
                "case_id": case_id,
                "task_id": task_id,
                "objective": objective,
                "status": "POLICY_BLOCKED",
                "error": str(exc),
                "privacy_flags": [
                    "AUTHORIZED_STATIC_ONLY",
                    "NO_MODEL_THEFT",
                    "NO_WEIGHT_EXTRACTION",
                    "NO_API_KEY_HARVESTING",
                    "NO_SYSTEM_PROMPT_EXTRACTION",
                    "NO_JAILBREAK_AUTOMATION",
                    "NO_POISONING_ENABLEMENT",
                ],
            }

        logger.info("Starting AIINT case=%s task=%s", case_id, task_id)

        evidences: List[EvidenceRef] = []
        entities: List[Entity] = []

        entity_specs = [
            ("ai_companies", "AI_COMPANY", "provider_docs"),
            ("model_developers", "MODEL_DEVELOPER", "official_model_card"),
            ("model_providers", "MODEL_PROVIDER", "provider_docs"),
            ("model_families", "MODEL_FAMILY", "official_model_card"),
            ("products", "PRODUCT", "provider_docs"),
            ("applications", "APPLICATION", "authorized_internal_inventory"),
            ("apis", "API", "api_docs"),
            ("deployments", "DEPLOYMENT", "authorized_internal_inventory"),

class PolicyViolation(Exception):
    pass


PROHIBITED_SCOPE_FLAGS = (
    "allow_model_theft",
    "allow_weight_extraction",
    "allow_api_key_harvesting",
    "allow_stolen_api_key_use",
    "allow_system_prompt_extraction",
    "allow_safeguard_bypass",
    "allow_jailbreak_automation",
    "allow_training_data_theft",
    "allow_private_dataset_reconstruction",
    "allow_memorized_personal_data_extraction",
    "allow_model_poisoning",
    "allow_dataset_poisoning",
    "allow_rag_poisoning",
    "allow_agent_memory_poisoning",
    "allow_malicious_model_artifact",
    "allow_model_repository_compromise",
    "allow_ml_pipeline_compromise",
    "allow_inference_server_compromise",
    "allow_gpu_cloud_attack",
    "allow_harmful_agent_deployment",
    "allow_deepfake_abuse_pipeline",
    "allow_unauthorized_biometric_surveillance",
    "allow_billing_circumvention",
    "allow_rate_limit_circumvention",
    "allow_unauthorized_model_access",
)

PROHIBITED_OBJECTIVE_KEYWORDS = (
    "steal model",
    "extract weights",
    "bypass model access",
    "stolen api key",
    "harvest api key",
    "extract system prompt",
    "jailbreak automation",
    "remove safety controls",
    "harmful deployment",
    "exfiltrate training data",
    "reconstruct private dataset",
    "memorized personal information",
    "poison model",
    "poison dataset",
    "poison rag",
    "poison memory",
    "malicious model",
    "compromise model repository",
    "compromise ml pipeline",
    "compromise inference server",
    "attack gpu",
    "attack cloud",
    "harmful autonomous agent",
    "deepfake abuse",
    "biometric surveillance",
)


def enforce_policy(objective: str, scope: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    if not isinstance(scope, dict):
        raise PolicyViolation("POLICY_BLOCKED: scope must be a dictionary.")

    mode = str(scope.get("mode", "AUTHORIZED_STATIC")).upper()
    allowed_modes = {
        "AUTHORIZED_STATIC",
        "AUTHORIZED_READONLY",
        "AUTHORIZED_EVALUATION_INGEST",
    }

    if mode not in allowed_modes:
        raise PolicyViolation(
            "POLICY_BLOCKED: AIINT requires lawful public/authorized static or read-only intelligence mode."
        )

    if mode == "AUTHORIZED_EVALUATION_INGEST":
        if not scope.get("authorized_evaluation_ingest", False):
            raise PolicyViolation(
                "POLICY_BLOCKED: evaluation ingest requires explicit authorization and must remain defensive."
            )

    for flag in PROHIBITED_SCOPE_FLAGS:
        if scope.get(flag, False):
            raise PolicyViolation(f"POLICY_BLOCKED: prohibited scope flag '{flag}'.")

    objective_lower = (objective or "").lower()
    for keyword in PROHIBITED_OBJECTIVE_KEYWORDS:
        if keyword in objective_lower:
            raise PolicyViolation(
                f"POLICY_BLOCKED: objective contains prohibited concept '{keyword}'."
            )

@dataclass
class EvidenceRef:
    evidence_id: str = ""
    case_id: str = ""
    source_id: str = ""
    upstream_source_id: str = ""
    source_type: str = "unknown"
    kind: str = ""
    observed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    reliability: float = 0.5
    limitations: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Entity:
    entity_id: str = ""
    entity_type: str = "UNKNOWN"
    name: Optional[str] = None
    aliases: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    attributes: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    evidence_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    confidence: float = 0.5
    limitations: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    privacy_flags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Relationship:
    relationship_id: str = ""
    source_id: str = ""
    target_id: str = ""
    relation: str = ""
    confidence: float = 0.5
    evidence_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    valid_from: Optional[datetime] = None
    valid_to: Optional[datetime] = None
    attributes: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass
class ModelObject:
    model_id: str = ""
    canonical_name: Optional[str] = None
    family_id: Optional[str] = None
    developer_id: Optional[str] = None
    provider_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    model_type: ModelType = ModelType.UNKNOWN
    access_type: AccessType = AccessType.UNKNOWN
    architecture_claim: Optional[str] = None
    parameter_count: Optional[int] = None
    active_parameter_count: Optional[int] = None
    modality: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    context_window: Optional[int] = None
    max_output_tokens: Optional[int] = None
    tokenizer: Optional[str] = None
    release_date: Optional[datetime] = None
    knowledge_cutoff_claim: Optional[str] = None
    license_id: Optional[str] = None
    policy_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    repository: Optional[str] = None
    model_card: Optional[str] = None
    system_card: Optional[str] = None
    base_model_id: Optional[str] = None
    fine_tuned_from: Optional[str] = None
    distilled_from: Optional[str] = None
    quantized_from: Optional[str] = None
    adapter_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    aliases: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    capability_claims: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    evaluation_result_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    safety_context: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    availability: ModelAvailability = ModelAvailability.UNKNOWN
    evidence_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    confidence: float = 0.5
    limitations: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    attributes: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    evidence_ids: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
