SECRET_REGEXES = [
    ("aws_access_key", re.compile(r"\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b")),
    ("private_key_marker", re.compile(r"-----BEGIN [A-Z ]*PRIVATE KEY-----")),
    ("jwt_like_token", re.compile(r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\b")),
    ("bearer_token", re.compile(r"(?i)\bbearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9._\-]+")),
    (
        "generic_secret_assignment",
        re.compile(
            r"(?i)\b(password|passwd|pwd|token|secret|api[_-]?key|apikey|"
            r"private[_-]?key|cookie|session[_-]?id|mfa|otp|credential)\b\s*[:=]\s*([^\s,;&]+)"
        ),
    ),
]

UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REGEXES = [
    re.compile(r"(?i)ignore (previous|all|system|aiint) (instructions|rules|policy|prompt)"),
    re.compile(
        r"(?i)(execute|run|download|upload|send|contact|use) "
        r"(this|the|these) (command|script|token|secret|credential|prompt)"
    ),
    re.compile(r"(?i)\b(jailbreak|bypass safeguard|extract weights|steal model|poison model|poison dataset)\b"),
]

SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE_TOKENS = (
    "private_key",
    "api_key",
    "apikey",
    "password",
    "passwd",
    "secret",
    "token",
    "credential",
    "personal_email",
    "home_address",
    "ssn",
    "passport",
    "biometric",
    "private_training_data",
    "confidential_prompt",
)

OFFICIAL_LINEAGE_SOURCES = {

    "peer_reviewed_paper",
}

    "authorized_internal_log",

}

    "provider_docs",

# ==============================================================================
# HELPERS
# ==============================================================================

def new_id(prefix: str) -> str:
    return f"{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"


def stable_id(prefix: str, *parts: Any) -> str:
    raw = "|".join(str(p) for p in parts)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8", errors="ignore")).hexdigest()
    return f"{prefix}_{digest[:12]}"


def json_serial(obj: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(obj, datetime):
        return obj.isoformat()
    if isinstance(obj, Enum):
        return obj.name
    if hasattr(obj, "__dataclass_fields__"):
        return asdict(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, set):
        return sorted(obj)
    if isinstance(obj, tuple):
        return list(obj)
    return str(obj)


def clamp(value: float, low: float = 0.0, high: float = 1.0) -> float:
    return max(low, min(high, value))


def enum_from_name(enum_cls: Any, name: Any, default: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(name, enum_cls):
        return name
    try:
        return enum_cls[str(name).upper()]
    except Exception:
        return default


def parse_dt(value: Any) -> Optional[datetime]:
    if value is None:
        return None

    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        if value.tzinfo is None:
            return value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return value.astimezone(timezone.utc)

    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text:
        return None

    text = text.replace("Z", "+00:00")

    try:
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(text)
        if dt.tzinfo is None:
            dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
        pass

    for fmt in (
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z",
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S.%f%z",
        "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
        "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M",
        "%Y-%m-%d",
    ):
        try:
            dt = datetime.strptime(text, fmt)
            if dt.tzinfo is None:
                dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            return dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
        except ValueError:
            continue

    return None


def to_int(value: Any) -> Optional[int]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return int(value)
    except Exception:
        return None


def to_float(value: Any) -> Optional[float]:
    if value is None:
        return None
    try:
        return float(value)
    except Exception:
        return None


def normalize_identifier(value: Any) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", str(value or "").strip().lower())


def process_text(text: Optional[str]) -> Tuple[Optional[str], List[str]]:
    if text is None:
        return None, []

    s = str(text)
    flags: List[str] = []

    for _, rx in SECRET_REGEXES:
        if rx.search(s):
            s = rx.sub("[REDACTED_SECRET]", s)
            flags.append("SECRET_REDACTED")

    for rx in UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REGEXES:
        if rx.search(s):
            s = rx.sub("[UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REDACTED]", s)
            flags.append("UNTRUSTED_INSTRUCTION_REDACTED")

    return s, sorted(set(flags))


def sanitize_mapping(
    mapping: Dict[str, Any],
    context: str = "attributes",
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    flags: List[str] = []

    for key, value in (mapping or {}).items():
        lk = str(key).lower()

        if any(token in lk for token in SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE_TOKENS):
            out[key] = "[REDACTED_SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE]"
            flags.append(f"SENSITIVE_ATTRIBUTE_REDACTED:{context}.{key}")
            continue

        if isinstance(value, str):
            red, f = process_text(value)
            out[key] = red
            flags.extend(f)
        elif isinstance(value, dict):
            sub, f = sanitize_mapping(value, f"{context}.{key}")
            out[key] = sub
            flags.extend(f)
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            lst: List[Any] = []
            for idx, item in enumerate(value):
                if isinstance(item, str):
                    red, f = process_text(item)
                    lst.append(red)
                    flags.extend(f)
                elif isinstance(item, dict):
                    sub, f = sanitize_mapping(item, f"{context}.{key}[{idx}]")
                    lst.append(sub)
                    flags.extend(f)
                else:
                    lst.append(item)
            out[key] = lst
        elif isinstance(value, set):
            out[key] = sorted(value)
        elif isinstance(value, tuple):
            out[key] = list(value)
        else:
            out[key] = value

    return out, sorted(set(flags))


def split_attributes(
    item: Dict[str, Any],
    extra_meta: Optional[Set[str]] = None,
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Any], List[str]]:
    exclude = META_KEYS | set(extra_meta or set()) | {"attributes"}
    extra, flags1 = sanitize_mapping(
        {k: v for k, v in item.items() if k not in exclude},
        "extra_attributes",
    )
    provided, flags2 = sanitize_mapping(
        item.get("attributes", {}) or {},
        "attributes",
    )
    return {**extra, **provided}, sorted(set(flags1 + flags2))


def make_evidence(
    item: Dict[str, Any],
    case_id: str,
    kind: str,
    now: datetime,
    default_source_type: str = "unknown",
) -> EvidenceRef:
    source_type = str(item.get("source_type") or default_source_type)
    reliability = float(item.get("reliability", SOURCE_RELIABILITY.get(source_type, 0.20)))

    observed_at = parse_dt(
        item.get("observed_at")
        or item.get("date")
        or item.get("released_at")
        or item.get("release_date")
        or item.get("published_at")
        or item.get("disclosure_date")
        or item.get("effective_date")
        or item.get("effective_at")
        or item.get("announced_at")
        or item.get("created_at")
        or item.get("first_seen")
        or item.get("last_seen")
    )

    limitations: List[str] = list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])

    if observed_at is None:
        limitations.append("OBSERVATION_TIME_UNRESOLVED")

    if source_type in {"marketing_claim", "social_post", "anonymous_claim", "unknown"}:
        limitations.append("LOWER_SOURCE_AUTHORITY")

    ev = EvidenceRef(
        evidence_id=new_id("EVID"),
        case_id=case_id,
        source_id=str(item.get("source_id") or "UNKNOWN_SOURCE"),
        upstream_source_id=str(item.get("upstream_source_id") or item.get("source_id") or "UNKNOWN_UPSTREAM"),
        source_type=source_type,
        kind=kind,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        reliability=clamp(reliability),
        limitations=sorted(set(limitations)),
    )

    item.setdefault("evidence_ids", [])
    item["evidence_ids"].append(ev.evidence_id)

    item.setdefault("confidence", ev.reliability)
    item.setdefault("limitations", [])
    item["limitations"] = sorted(set(list(item["limitations"]) + ev.limitations))

    return ev


def default_entity_limitations(entity_type: str) -> List[str]:
    t = entity_type.upper()
    base: List[str] = []

    if t == "API":
        base.append("API alias may be mutable and may route to different snapshots or regional variants.")
    elif t == "PRODUCT":
        base.append("Product capability may include retrieval, tools, guardrails, UI, and workflow logic beyond the model.")
    elif t == "APPLICATION":
        base.append("Application behavior depends on prompts, tools, data access, and orchestration.")
    elif t == "DEPLOYMENT":
        base.append("Deployment behavior may differ from base model due to quantization, system prompt, RAG, tools, and policy.")
    elif t == "AGENT":
        base.append("Agent capability derives from model, tools, permissions, memory, orchestrator, and environment.")
    elif t == "DATASET":
        base.append("Dataset provenance, filtering, deduplication, and training use may be partially disclosed.")
    elif t == "BENCHMARK":
        base.append("Benchmark scores are protocol, version, prompting, tool, sampling, evaluator, and date specific.")
    elif t == "LICENSE":
        base.append("License text requires legal interpretation for consequential deployment or redistribution.")
    elif t == "GUARDRAIL":
        base.append("Guardrail presence does not prove effectiveness without evaluation evidence.")
    elif t == "MONITORING":
        base.append("Observability coverage may be partial and version-specific.")

    return base


# ==============================================================================
# PARSERS
# ==============================================================================

def parse_entities(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    entity_type: str,
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
    default_source_type: str = "provider_docs",
) -> List[Entity]:
    out: List[Entity] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, entity_type.lower(), now, default_source_type)
        evidences.append(ev)

        entity_id = str(
            item.get("entity_id")
            or item.get("id")
            or stable_id(entity_type, item.get("name") or new_id("ENT"))
        )

        attributes, flags = split_attributes(item)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + default_entity_limitations(entity_type)
                + flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            Entity(
                entity_id=entity_id,
                entity_type=entity_type.upper(),
                name=item.get("name"),
                aliases=[str(x) for x in item.get("aliases", []) or []],
                attributes=attributes,
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                privacy_flags=flags,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_relationships(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Relationship]:
    out: List[Relationship] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "relationship", now, "provider_docs")
        evidences.append(ev)

        attributes, _ = split_attributes(item, REL_FIELD_KEYS)

        out.append(
            Relationship(
                relationship_id=str(
                    item.get("relationship_id")
                    or stable_id("REL", item.get("source_id"), item.get("target_id"), item.get("relation"))
                ),
                source_id=str(item.get("source_id") or ""),
                target_id=str(item.get("target_id") or ""),
                relation=str(item.get("relation") or "RELATED_TO").upper(),
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                valid_from=parse_dt(item.get("valid_from")),
                valid_to=parse_dt(item.get("valid_to")),
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_models(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[ModelObject]:
    out: List[ModelObject] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "model", now, "official_model_card")
        evidences.append(ev)

        capability_claims: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        claim_flags: List[str] = []

        for claim in item.get("capability_claims", []) or []:
            if isinstance(claim, str):
                red, f = process_text(claim)
                claim_flags.extend(f)
                capability_claims.append(
                    {
                        "claim": red,
                        "claim_type": CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED.name,
                        "evidence_ids": [ev.evidence_id],
                    }
                )
            elif isinstance(claim, dict):
                sanitized, f = sanitize_mapping(claim, "model.capability_claim")
                claim_flags.extend(f)
                sanitized.setdefault("claim_type", CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED.name)
                sanitized.setdefault("evidence_ids", [ev.evidence_id])
                capability_claims.append(sanitized)

        safety_context, safety_flags = sanitize_mapping(item.get("safety_context", {}) or {}, "model.safety_context")
        attributes, attr_flags = split_attributes(item, MODEL_FIELD_KEYS)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Model metadata may be provider-claimed and not independently verified.",
                    "Open weights does not automatically mean open source.",
                    "Parameter count is not a capability score.",
                    "Maximum context window is not equivalent to effective reasoning quality across full context.",
                    "Model card is not independent verification.",
                ]
                + claim_flags
                + safety_flags
                + attr_flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            ModelObject(
                model_id=str(item.get("model_id") or stable_id("MODEL", item.get("canonical_name") or item.get("name"))),
                canonical_name=item.get("canonical_name") or item.get("name"),
                family_id=item.get("family_id"),
                developer_id=item.get("developer_id"),
                provider_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("provider_ids", []) or []],
                model_type=enum_from_name(ModelType, item.get("model_type"), ModelType.UNKNOWN),
                access_type=enum_from_name(AccessType, item.get("access_type"), AccessType.UNKNOWN),
                architecture_claim=item.get("architecture_claim"),
                parameter_count=to_int(item.get("parameter_count")),
                active_parameter_count=to_int(item.get("active_parameter_count")),
                modality=[str(x) for x in item.get("modality", []) or []],
                context_window=to_int(item.get("context_window")),
                max_output_tokens=to_int(item.get("max_output_tokens")),
                tokenizer=item.get("tokenizer"),
                release_date=parse_dt(item.get("release_date")),
                knowledge_cutoff_claim=item.get("knowledge_cutoff_claim"),
                license_id=item.get("license_id"),
                policy_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("policy_ids", []) or []],
                repository=item.get("repository"),
                model_card=item.get("model_card"),
                system_card=item.get("system_card"),
                base_model_id=item.get("base_model_id"),
                fine_tuned_from=item.get("fine_tuned_from"),
                distilled_from=item.get("distilled_from"),
                quantized_from=item.get("quantized_from"),
                adapter_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("adapter_ids", []) or []],
                aliases=[str(x) for x in item.get("aliases", []) or []],
                capability_claims=capability_claims,
                evaluation_result_ids=[str(x) for x in item.get("evaluation_result_ids", []) or []],
                safety_context=safety_context,
                availability=enum_from_name(ModelAvailability, item.get("availability"), ModelAvailability.UNKNOWN),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_benchmark_results(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[BenchmarkResult]:
    out: List[BenchmarkResult] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "benchmark_result", now, "provider_benchmark")
        evidences.append(ev)

        tools = item.get("tools")
        tools_list: Optional[List[str]] = None
        if tools is not None:
            tools_list = [str(x) for x in tools or []]

        protocol_fields = [
            item.get("benchmark_id"),
            item.get("benchmark_version"),
            item.get("metric"),
            item.get("prompting"),
            item.get("shots"),
            tools,
            item.get("sampling"),
            item.get("evaluator"),
            item.get("date") or item.get("published_at"),
        ]

        protocol_complete = all(x is not None for x in protocol_fields)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Benchmark score is not real-world performance proof.",
                    "Scores are comparable only under normalized protocol, version, prompting, tools, sampling, evaluator, and date context.",
                    "Provider-reported benchmark is not independent evaluation.",
                ]
            )
        )

        out.append(
            BenchmarkResult(
                result_id=str(
                    item.get("result_id")
                    or stable_id("BMRES", item.get("model_id"), item.get("benchmark_id"), item.get("score"), ev.source_id)
                ),
                model_id=item.get("model_id"),
                model_version=item.get("model_version"),
                benchmark_id=item.get("benchmark_id"),
                benchmark_version=item.get("benchmark_version"),
                score=to_float(item.get("score")),
                metric=item.get("metric"),
                prompting=item.get("prompting"),
                shots=to_int(item.get("shots")),
                tools=tools_list,
                sampling=item.get("sampling"),
                evaluator=item.get("evaluator"),
                source_type=str(item.get("source_type") or ev.source_type),
                independence_group=str(item.get("independence_group") or ev.upstream_source_id),
                date=parse_dt(item.get("date") or item.get("published_at")),
                protocol_complete=protocol_complete,
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_security_findings(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[SecurityFinding]:
    out: List[SecurityFinding] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "security_finding", now, "security_advisory")
        evidences.append(ev)

        attributes, flags = split_attributes(item, SEC_FIELD_KEYS)
        affected_type = str(item.get("affected_entity_type") or "unknown").lower()

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Security issue class is not verified exploitation.",
                    "Product/API/deployment finding does not automatically establish base model vulnerability.",
                    "Mitigation guidance is defensive and does not provide exploit procedures.",
                ]
                + flags
            )
        )

        if affected_type in {"product", "application", "api", "deployment", "agent"}:
            limitations.append("Affected layer is not base model; base-model impact remains unresolved.")

        out.append(
            SecurityFinding(
                finding_id=str(
                    item.get("finding_id")
                    or stable_id("SEC", item.get("title"), item.get("affected_entity_id"), item.get("affected_version"))
                ),
                title=item.get("title"),
                issue_type=enum_from_name(SecurityIssueType, item.get("issue_type"), SecurityIssueType.UNKNOWN),
                affected_entity_type=affected_type,
                affected_entity_id=item.get("affected_entity_id"),
                affected_version=item.get("affected_version"),
                deployment_context=item.get("deployment_context"),
                disclosure_date=parse_dt(item.get("disclosure_date")),
                severity_context=item.get("severity_context"),
                exploitability_context=item.get("exploitability_context"),
                mitigation=item.get("mitigation"),
                fixed_version=item.get("fixed_version"),
                verification_state=enum_from_name(VerificationState, item.get("verification_state"), VerificationState.INCONCLUSIVE),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_incidents(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Incident]:
    out: List[Incident] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "incident", now, "ai_incident_database")
        evidences.append(ev)

        attributes, flags = split_attributes(item, INC_FIELD_KEYS)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Incident claim is not verified root cause.",
                    "Provider outage does not prove every customer was impacted.",
                    "Model incident does not prove every deployment used the affected version.",
                ]
                + flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            Incident(
                incident_id=str(
                    item.get("incident_id")
                    or stable_id("INC", item.get("system_id"), item.get("date"), item.get("incident_type"))
                ),
                system_id=item.get("system_id"),
                model_id=item.get("model_id"),
                provider_id=item.get("provider_id"),
                date=parse_dt(item.get("date")),
                incident_type=enum_from_name(IncidentType, item.get("incident_type"), IncidentType.UNKNOWN),
                impact=item.get("impact"),
                affected_versions=[str(x) for x in item.get("affected_versions", []) or []],
                cause_claim=item.get("cause_claim"),
                official_status=item.get("official_status"),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_pricing(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Pricing]:
    out: List[Pricing] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "pricing", now, "pricing_page")
        evidences.append(ev)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Pricing is time-specific and may change.",
                    "Token price is not total deployment cost.",
                ]
            )
        )

        out.append(
            Pricing(
                pricing_id=str(item.get("pricing_id") or stable_id("PRICE", item.get("entity_id"), item.get("effective_date"))),
                entity_id=item.get("entity_id"),
                currency=item.get("currency"),
                input_token_price_per_million=to_float(item.get("input_token_price_per_million")),
                output_token_price_per_million=to_float(item.get("output_token_price_per_million")),
                cached_token_price_per_million=to_float(item.get("cached_token_price_per_million")),
                effective_date=parse_dt(item.get("effective_date")),
                expiration_date=parse_dt(item.get("expiration_date")),
                source=item.get("source") or ev.source_id,
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_change_events(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[ChangeEvent]:
    out: List[ChangeEvent] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "change_event", now, "release_notes")
        evidences.append(ev)

        old_state_wrapped, old_flags = sanitize_mapping({"value": item.get("old_state")}, "change.old_state")
        new_state_wrapped, new_flags = sanitize_mapping({"value": item.get("new_state")}, "change.new_state")

        attributes, attr_flags = split_attributes(item, CHANGE_FIELD_KEYS)

        limitations = sorted(
            set(
                list(item.get("limitations", []) or [])
                + [
                    "Change is not automatically improvement.",
                    "Alias route changes may affect reproducibility and auditability.",
                ]
                + old_flags
                + new_flags
                + attr_flags
            )
        )

        out.append(
            ChangeEvent(
                change_id=str(
                    item.get("change_id")
                    or stable_id("CHG", item.get("entity_id"), item.get("change_type"), item.get("effective_at"))
                ),
                entity_id=item.get("entity_id"),
                change_type=enum_from_name(ChangeType, item.get("change_type"), ChangeType.UNKNOWN),
                old_state=old_state_wrapped.get("value"),
                new_state=new_state_wrapped.get("value"),
                effective_at=parse_dt(item.get("effective_at")),
                announced_at=parse_dt(item.get("announced_at")),
                source=item.get("source") or ev.source_id,
                impact_candidate=item.get("impact_candidate"),
                evidence_ids=[ev.evidence_id],
                confidence=clamp(float(item.get("confidence", ev.reliability))),
                limitations=limitations,
                attributes=attributes,
            )
        )

    return out


def parse_cost_usage(
    items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    case_id: str,
    now: datetime,
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for item in items or []:
        ev = make_evidence(item, case_id, "cost_usage", now, "authorized_internal_log")
        evidences.append(ev)

        sanitized, flags = sanitize_mapping(item, "cost_usage")
        sanitized["evidence_ids"] = [ev.evidence_id]
        sanitized["limitations"] = sorted(set(list(item.get("limitations", []) or []) + flags))
        out.append(sanitized)

    return out

# ANALYSIS

def build_indexes(
    entities: List[Entity],
    models: List[ModelObject],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    evidences: List[EvidenceRef],
) -> Tuple[
    Dict[str, Entity],
    Dict[str, Set[str]],
    Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    Dict[str, EvidenceRef],
]:
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity] = {}

    for e in entities:
        entity_index[e.entity_id] = e

    for m in models:
        if m.model_id not in entity_index:
            entity_index[m.model_id] = Entity(
                entity_id=m.model_id,
                entity_type="MODEL",
                name=m.canonical_name,
                aliases=m.aliases,
                attributes={
                    "model_type": m.model_type.name,
                    "access_type": m.access_type.name,
                    "family_id": m.family_id,
                    "developer_id": m.developer_id,
                    "provider_ids": m.provider_ids,
                    "license_id": m.license_id,
                    "context_window": m.context_window,
                    "parameter_count": m.parameter_count,
                    "tool_use": m.attributes.get("tool_use"),
                    "function_calling": m.attributes.get("function_calling"),
                    "supported_interfaces": m.attributes.get("supported_interfaces", []),
                },
                evidence_ids=m.evidence_ids,
                confidence=m.confidence,
                limitations=m.limitations,
            )

    alias_to_ids: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)

    for e in entity_index.values():
        if e.name:
            alias_to_ids[normalize_identifier(e.name)].add(e.entity_id)
        for alias in e.aliases:
            alias_to_ids[normalize_identifier(alias)].add(e.entity_id)
        alias_to_ids[normalize_identifier(e.entity_id)].add(e.entity_id)

    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)

    for r in relationships:
        rel_by_source[r.source_id].append(r)
        rel_by_target[r.target_id].append(r)

    evidence_by_id = {ev.evidence_id: ev for ev in evidences}

    return entity_index, alias_to_ids, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, evidence_by_id


def resolve_references(
    references: List[Any],
    alias_to_ids: Dict[str, Set[str]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for ref in references or []:
        if isinstance(ref, str):
            query = ref
            expected_type = None
        elif isinstance(ref, dict):
            query = str(ref.get("reference") or ref.get("name") or ref.get("id") or "")
            expected_type = ref.get("expected_type")
        else:
            query = str(ref)
            expected_type = None

        norm = normalize_identifier(query)
        candidates = set(alias_to_ids.get(norm, set()))

        if expected_type:
            et = str(expected_type).upper()
            candidates = {cid for cid in candidates if entity_index.get(cid) and entity_index[cid].entity_type == et}

        resolved_ids = sorted(candidates)
        primary_id = resolved_ids[0] if resolved_ids else None
        layer = entity_index[primary_id].entity_type if primary_id in entity_index else "UNRESOLVED"

        out.append(
            {
                "reference": query,
                "expected_type": expected_type,
                "resolved_ids": resolved_ids,
                "primary_id": primary_id,
                "layer": layer,
                "ambiguous": len(resolved_ids) > 1,
                "evidence_ids": entity_index[primary_id].evidence_ids if primary_id in entity_index else [],
                "limitations": [
                    "Reference resolution is alias-based and may be ambiguous.",
                    "API/product/model/deployment/agent layers must remain separate.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def lineage_confidence_from_evidence(
    evidence_ids: List[str],
    evidence_by_id: Dict[str, EvidenceRef],
) -> LineageConfidence:
    types = {evidence_by_id[eid].source_type for eid in evidence_ids if eid in evidence_by_id}

    if types & OFFICIAL_LINEAGE_SOURCES:
        return LineageConfidence.OFFICIAL
    if types & SUPPORTED_LINEAGE_SOURCES:
        return LineageConfidence.SUPPORTED
    if types & PROBABLE_LINEAGE_SOURCES:
        return LineageConfidence.PROBABLE
    return LineageConfidence.UNKNOWN


def build_lineage(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    evidence_by_id: Dict[str, EvidenceRef],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    seen: Set[Tuple[str, str, str]] = set()

    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in relationships:
        rel_by_source[r.source_id].append(r)

    lineage_relations = {
        "BASE_MODEL_OF",
        "FINE_TUNED_FROM",
        "DISTILLED_FROM",
        "QUANTIZED_FROM",
        "USES_ADAPTER",
        "VERSION_OF",
        "DERIVED_FROM",
        "BASED_ON",
    }

    def add_edge(source: str, target: str, relation: str, confidence: float, evidence_ids: List[str], source_obj: str) -> None:
        if not source or not target:
            return
        key = (source, target, relation)
        if key in seen:
            return
        seen.add(key)
        out.append(
            {
                "lineage_id": new_id("LIN"),
                "source_model_id": source,
                "target_model_id": target,
                "relation": relation,
                "confidence": confidence,
                "lineage_confidence": lineage_confidence_from_evidence(evidence_ids, evidence_by_id).name,
                "evidence_ids": sorted(set(evidence_ids)),
                "source_object": source_obj,
                "limitations": [
                    "Lineage confidence reflects evidence strength, not behavioral equivalence.",
                    "Fine-tune/distill/quantize relationships do not imply capability or safety equivalence.",
                ],
            }
        )

    for m in models:
        if m.base_model_id:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.base_model_id, "BASED_ON", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        if m.fine_tuned_from:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.fine_tuned_from, "FINE_TUNED_FROM", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        if m.distilled_from:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.distilled_from, "DISTILLED_FROM", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        if m.quantized_from:
            add_edge(m.model_id, m.quantized_from, "QUANTIZED_FROM", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)
        for adapter_id in m.adapter_ids:
            add_edge(m.model_id, adapter_id, "USES_ADAPTER", m.confidence, m.evidence_ids, m.model_id)

        for r in rel_by_source.get(m.model_id, []):
            if r.relation in lineage_relations:
                add_edge(r.source_id, r.target_id, r.relation, r.confidence, r.evidence_ids + m.evidence_ids, r.relationship_id)

    return out


def assess_capability(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    results_by_model: Dict[str, List[BenchmarkResult]] = defaultdict(list)

    for br in benchmark_results:
        if br.model_id:
            results_by_model[br.model_id].append(br)

    for m in models:
        claims: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for claim in m.capability_claims:
            claim_type = str(claim.get("claim_type") or CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED.name).upper()
            claims.append(
                {
                    "claim": claim.get("claim"),
                    "claim_type": claim_type,
                    "evidence_ids": claim.get("evidence_ids", []),
                    "limitations": [
                        "Provider claim is not independent measurement.",
                        "Capability does not establish reliability or safety.",
                    ],
                }
            )

        measured: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for br in results_by_model.get(m.model_id, []):
            if br.source_type in INDEPENDENT_EVAL_SOURCES:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.INDEPENDENTLY_MEASURED
            elif br.source_type in INTERNAL_EVAL_SOURCES:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.AUTHORIZED_INTERNAL_MEASUREMENT
            elif br.source_type in PROVIDER_CLAIM_SOURCES:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.PROVIDER_CLAIMED
            else:
                ctype = CapabilityClaimType.UNKNOWN

            measured.append(
                {
                    "result_id": br.result_id,
                    "benchmark_id": br.benchmark_id,
                    "benchmark_version": br.benchmark_version,
                    "score": br.score,
                    "metric": br.metric,
                    "claim_type": ctype.name,
                    "comparability_status": br.comparability_status.name,
                    "evidence_ids": br.evidence_ids,
                    "limitations": br.limitations,
                }
            )

        out.append(
            {
                "model_id": m.model_id,
                "canonical_name": m.canonical_name,
                "claims": claims,
                "measurements": measured,
                "assessment": "CLAIMS_AND_MEASUREMENTS_SEPARATED",
                "limitations": [
                    "Capability is not reliability.",
                    "Capability is not safety.",
                    "Benchmark performance is task/distribution/protocol specific.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def normalize_benchmark_results(
    benchmark_results: List[BenchmarkResult],
) -> Tuple[Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    groups: Dict[str, List[BenchmarkResult]] = defaultdict(list)
    conflicts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for br in benchmark_results:
        if br.protocol_complete:
            group_key = stable_id(
                "BMGROUP",
                br.benchmark_id,
                br.benchmark_version,
                br.metric,
                br.prompting,
                br.shots,
                tuple(br.tools or []),
                br.sampling,
                br.evaluator,
            )
            br.comparability_group = group_key
            br.comparability_status = BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABLE
            groups[group_key].append(br)
        else:
            group_key = f"LIMITED::{br.result_id}"
            br.comparability_group = group_key
            br.comparability_status = BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED
            groups[group_key].append(br)

    group_objects: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    for gid, items in groups.items():
        scores = [x.score for x in items if x.score is not None]
        conflict = False

        if len(scores) > 1:
            mean = statistics.mean(scores)
            tolerance = max(1.0, abs(mean) * 0.02)
            if max(scores) - min(scores) > tolerance:
                conflict = True
                for x in items:
                    x.comparability_status = BenchmarkComparability.NOT_DIRECTLY_COMPARABLE
                conflicts.append(
                    {
                        "group_id": gid,
                        "result_ids": [x.result_id for x in items],
                        "scores": scores,
                        "description": "Same protocol group produced materially different scores.",
                    }
                )

        group_objects[gid] = {
            "group_id": gid,
            "result_ids": [x.result_id for x in items],
            "protocol_complete": bool(items and items[0].protocol_complete),
            "conflict": conflict,
            "comparability_status": items[0].comparability_status.name if items else BenchmarkComparability.UNKNOWN.name,
        }

    return group_objects, conflicts


def get_related_models(
    entity_id: Optional[str],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    depth: int = 2,
) -> Set[str]:
    if not entity_id:
        return set()

    relations = {
        "USES_MODEL",
        "PRIMARY_MODEL_OF",
        "SERVES_MODEL",
        "DEPLOYS_MODEL",
        "ROUTES_TO",
        "USES_API",
        "USES_PRODUCT",
        "USES_DEPLOYMENT",
        "BASED_ON",
        "VERSION_OF",
        "FALLBACK_MODEL_OF",
    }

    visited: Set[str] = set()
    frontier: Set[str] = {entity_id}
    models: Set[str] = set()

    for _ in range(depth):
        next_frontier: Set[str] = set()

        for node in frontier:
            if node in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(node)

            ent = entity_index.get(node)
            if ent and ent.entity_type == "MODEL":
                models.add(node)

            for r in rel_by_source.get(node, []):
                if r.relation in relations:
                    next_frontier.add(r.target_id)

            for r in rel_by_target.get(node, []):
                if r.relation in relations:
                    next_frontier.add(r.source_id)

        frontier = next_frontier - visited

    return models


def map_security_layers(
    findings: List[SecurityFinding],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for f in findings:
        affected_id = f.affected_entity_id
        affected_type = (f.affected_entity_type or "unknown").upper()
        related_models = get_related_models(affected_id, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index, depth=2)

        if affected_type in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"}:
            layer = "MODEL"
            base_model_impact = "SUPPORTED" if f.verification_state == VerificationState.SUPPORTED else "PARTIAL"
        elif affected_type in {"PRODUCT", "APPLICATION", "API", "DEPLOYMENT", "AGENT"}:
            layer = affected_type
            if f.attributes.get("base_model_vulnerable") is True:
                base_model_impact = "CANDIDATE_REQUIRES_VALIDATION"
            else:
                base_model_impact = "NOT_ESTABLISHED"
        else:
            layer = affected_type or "UNKNOWN"
            base_model_impact = "UNKNOWN"

        out.append(
            {
                "finding_id": f.finding_id,
                "affected_layer": layer,
                "affected_entity_id": affected_id,
                "affected_version": f.affected_version,
                "related_model_ids": sorted(related_models),
                "base_model_impact": base_model_impact,
                "issue_type": f.issue_type.name,
                "mitigation": f.mitigation,
                "fixed_version": f.fixed_version,
                "evidence_ids": f.evidence_ids,
                "limitations": f.limitations,
            }
        )

    return out


def propagate_incidents(
    incidents: List[Incident],
    entities: List[Entity],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    deployment_like_types = {"DEPLOYMENT", "APPLICATION", "PRODUCT", "AGENT"}

    for inc in incidents:
        model_id = inc.model_id
        if not model_id and inc.system_id and entity_index.get(inc.system_id) and entity_index[inc.system_id].entity_type == "MODEL":
            model_id = inc.system_id

        affected_versions = set(inc.affected_versions or [])
        affected: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for ent in entities:
            if ent.entity_type not in deployment_like_types:
                continue

            related_models = get_related_models(ent.entity_id, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index, depth=2)

            if model_id and model_id not in related_models:
                continue

            version = ent.attributes.get("model_version") or ent.attributes.get("version")
            if isinstance(version, list):
                version = version[0] if version else None

            if affected_versions and version and str(version) in affected_versions:
                state = "DEPLOYED_DURING_WINDOW"
            elif not affected_versions:
                state = "POTENTIALLY_AFFECTED"
            elif version and str(version) not in affected_versions:
                state = "NOT_AFFECTED_CANDIDATE"
            else:
                state = "POTENTIALLY_AFFECTED"

            affected.append(
                {
                    "entity_id": ent.entity_id,
                    "entity_type": ent.entity_type,
                    "state": state,
                    "observed_version": version,
                    "evidence_ids": ent.evidence_ids + inc.evidence_ids,
                }
            )

        out.append(
            {
                "incident_id": inc.incident_id,
                "model_id": model_id,
                "affected_versions": sorted(affected_versions),
                "deployment_propagation": affected if affected else [{"state": "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE", "entity_id": None}],
                "limitations": [
                    "Incident propagation is version/deployment specific.",
                    "No deployment evidence does not prove no impact; it may reflect missing inventory.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def map_supply_chain(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    entities: List[Entity],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    rel_by_target: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    supply_relations = {
        "DEPENDS_ON",
        "RUNS_ON",
        "SERVED_BY",
        "HOSTED_BY",
        "USES_FRAMEWORK",
        "USES_PACKAGE",
        "USES_INFERENCE_ENGINE",
        "USES_CLOUD",
        "RUNS_ON_CLOUD",
        "RUNS_ON_HARDWARE",
        "RETRIEVES_FROM",
        "EMBEDDED_BY",
        "RERANKED_BY",
        "USES_VECTOR_DATABASE",
        "GUARDED_BY",
        "MONITORED_BY",
        "USES_PROVIDER",
        "PROVIDED_BY",
    }

    paths: List[List[str]] = []

    for m in models:
        visited: Set[str] = set()
        frontier: List[List[str]] = [[m.model_id]]

        while frontier:
            path = frontier.pop(0)
            node = path[-1]

            if node in visited:
                continue
            visited.add(node)

            if len(path) > 1:
                paths.append(path)

            if len(path) >= 6:
                continue

            for r in rel_by_source.get(node, []):
                if r.relation in supply_relations or r.relation.startswith(("USES_", "RUNS_ON_", "DEPENDS_ON_")):
                    frontier.append(path + [r.target_id])

    dep_type_map = {
        "USES_PROVIDER": "PROVIDER",
        "PROVIDED_BY": "PROVIDER",
        "HOSTED_BY": "PROVIDER",
        "USES_FRAMEWORK": "FRAMEWORK",
        "USES_PACKAGE": "PACKAGE",
        "DEPENDS_ON_PACKAGE": "PACKAGE",
        "USES_INFERENCE_ENGINE": "INFERENCE_ENGINE",
        "RUNS_ON_HARDWARE": "HARDWARE",
        "USES_CLOUD": "CLOUD",
        "RUNS_ON_CLOUD": "CLOUD",
        "RETRIEVES_FROM": "RAG",
        "EMBEDDED_BY": "EMBEDDING_MODEL",
        "RERANKED_BY": "RERANKER",
        "USES_VECTOR_DATABASE": "VECTOR_DATABASE",
        "GUARDED_BY": "GUARDRAIL",
        "MONITORED_BY": "MONITORING",
    }

    deployment_like_types = {"DEPLOYMENT", "APPLICATION", "PRODUCT", "AGENT"}
    dep_to_deployments: Dict[Tuple[str, str], Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)

    for ent in entities:
        if ent.entity_type not in deployment_like_types:
            continue

        for r in rel_by_source.get(ent.entity_id, []):
            dep_type = dep_type_map.get(r.relation)
            if dep_type:
                dep_to_deployments[(dep_type, r.target_id)].add(ent.entity_id)

        related_models = get_related_models(ent.entity_id, rel_by_source, rel_by_target, entity_index, depth=2)
        for mid in related_models:
            dep_to_deployments[("MODEL", mid)].add(ent.entity_id)

    common_mode = [
        {
            "dependency_type": dtype,
            "dependency_id": did,
            "deployment_ids": sorted(deps),
            "risk_note": "Shared dependency may create common-mode failure or correlated change risk.",
        }
        for (dtype, did), deps in dep_to_deployments.items()
        if len(deps) > 1
    ]

    return {
        "paths": paths[:200],
        "common_mode_dependencies": common_mode,
        "limitations": [
            "Supply-chain map is evidence-based and may be incomplete.",
            "Common-mode dependency is resilience context, not attack targeting.",
        ],
    }


def estimate_costs(
    pricing: List[Pricing],
    usage_items: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    now: datetime,
) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
    estimates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    missing: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    pricing_by_entity: Dict[str, List[Pricing]] = defaultdict(list)
    for p in pricing:
        if p.entity_id:
            pricing_by_entity[p.entity_id].append(p)

    def select_pricing(entity_id: Optional[str]) -> Optional[Pricing]:
        if not entity_id:
            return None
        candidates = pricing_by_entity.get(entity_id, [])
        if not candidates:
            return None

        active = [
            p
            for p in candidates
            if (p.effective_date is None or p.effective_date <= now)
            and (p.expiration_date is None or p.expiration_date >= now)
        ]

        pool = active or candidates
        return max(pool, key=lambda x: x.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc))

    def find_entity_for_usage(usage: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[str]:
        for key in ("pricing_entity_id", "api_id", "model_id", "provider_id", "deployment_id"):
            val = usage.get(key)
            if val:
                return str(val)
        return None

    for usage in usage_items or []:
        entity_id = find_entity_for_usage(usage)
        p = select_pricing(entity_id)

        input_tokens = to_float(usage.get("input_tokens")) or 0.0
        output_tokens = to_float(usage.get("output_tokens")) or 0.0
        cached_tokens = to_float(usage.get("cached_tokens")) or 0.0
        period_days = to_float(usage.get("period_days")) or 30.0

        if not p:
            missing.append(
                {
                    "usage_id": usage.get("usage_id") or new_id("USAGE"),
                    "entity_id": entity_id,
                    "reason": "No applicable pricing record found.",
                    "evidence_ids": usage.get("evidence_ids", []),
                }
            )
            continue

        input_cost = (input_tokens / 1_000_000.0) * (p.input_token_price_per_million or 0.0)
        output_cost = (output_tokens / 1_000_000.0) * (p.output_token_price_per_million or 0.0)
        cached_cost = (cached_tokens / 1_000_000.0) * (p.cached_token_price_per_million or 0.0)
        total = input_cost + output_cost + cached_cost

        estimates.append(
            {
                "estimate_id": new_id("COST"),
                "entity_id": p.entity_id,
                "pricing_id": p.pricing_id,
                "currency": p.currency,
                "input_tokens": input_tokens,
                "output_tokens": output_tokens,
                "cached_tokens": cached_tokens,
                "period_days": period_days,
                "input_cost": round(input_cost, 6),
                "output_cost": round(output_cost, 6),
                "cached_cost": round(cached_cost, 6),
                "total_cost": round(total, 6),
                "formula": "(input_tokens/1e6 * input_price_per_m) + (output_tokens/1e6 * output_price_per_m) + (cached_tokens/1e6 * cached_price_per_m)",
                "pricing_effective_date": p.effective_date,
                "limitations": [
                    "Deterministic arithmetic only.",
                    "Token price is not total infrastructure or engineering cost.",
                    "Pricing is time-specific.",
                ],
                "evidence_ids": sorted(set(usage.get("evidence_ids", []) + p.evidence_ids)),
            }
        )

    return estimates, missing


def assess_substitutability(
    models: List[ModelObject],
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    models_by_id = {m.model_id: m for m in models}

    primary_ids = [
        rr["primary_id"]
        for rr in resolved_references
        if rr.get("primary_id") and rr.get("layer") == "MODEL"
    ]

    if not primary_ids and models:
        primary_ids = [models[0].model_id]

    for pid in primary_ids:
        primary = models_by_id.get(pid)
        if not primary:
            continue

        candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for m in models:
            if m.model_id == pid:
                continue

            reasons: List[str] = []
            state = Substitutability.UNKNOWN

            same_family = bool(primary.family_id and primary.family_id == m.family_id)
            same_access = primary.access_type == m.access_type and primary.access_type != AccessType.UNKNOWN
            context_ok = (
                primary.context_window is not None
                and m.context_window is not None
                and m.context_window >= primary.context_window
            )
            interfaces_primary = set(primary.attributes.get("supported_interfaces", []) or [])
            interfaces_candidate = set(m.attributes.get("supported_interfaces", []) or [])
            interface_overlap = bool(interfaces_primary & interfaces_candidate)

            tool_primary = bool(primary.attributes.get("tool_use") or primary.attributes.get("function_calling"))
            tool_candidate = bool(m.attributes.get("tool_use") or m.attributes.get("function_calling"))

            if same_family and same_access and context_ok and (interface_overlap or tool_primary == tool_candidate):
                state = Substitutability.EASY
                reasons.append("Same family/access/context/tool profile.")
            elif primary.developer_id and primary.developer_id == m.developer_id:
                state = Substitutability.MODERATE
                reasons.append("Same developer but version/lineage/capability differences require evaluation.")
            elif set(primary.provider_ids) & set(m.provider_ids):
                state = Substitutability.MODERATE
                reasons.append("Same provider but model/version differences require evaluation.")
            else:
                state = Substitutability.DIFFICULT
                reasons.append("Different family/provider/access profile.")

            if not context_ok:
                reasons.append("Context-window equivalence unresolved.")
            if tool_primary != tool_candidate:
                reasons.append("Tool/function-calling profile differs.")

            candidates.append(
                {
                    "candidate_model_id": m.model_id,
                    "candidate_name": m.canonical_name,
                    "state": state.name,
                    "reasons": reasons,
                    "limitations": [
                        "Substitutability is not capability equivalence.",
                        "Migration requires task-specific evaluation, license review, and deployment testing.",
                    ],
                }
            )

        out.append(
            {
                "primary_model_id": pid,
                "primary_name": primary.canonical_name,
                "candidates": candidates,
            }
        )

    return out


def assess_lock_in(
    entities: List[Entity],
    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    out: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for ent in entities:
        if ent.entity_type not in {"DEPLOYMENT", "APPLICATION", "PRODUCT", "AGENT"}:
            continue

        factors: List[str] = []

        for r in rel_by_source.get(ent.entity_id, []):
            target = entity_index.get(r.target_id)
            if not target:
                continue

            if r.relation == "USES_API" and target.attributes.get("proprietary") is True:
                factors.append("Proprietary API dependency.")
            if r.relation in {"EMBEDDED_BY", "USES_VECTOR_DATABASE"}:
                factors.append("Embedding/vector-store coupling.")
            if r.relation == "USES_MODEL":
                model = entity_index.get(r.target_id)
                if model and model.attributes.get("fine_tuned") is True:
                    factors.append("Fine-tuned model coupling.")

        if ent.attributes.get("system_prompt_reference"):
            factors.append("System prompt coupling.")
        if ent.attributes.get("tool_schema_lock_in"):
            factors.append("Tool schema coupling.")
        if ent.attributes.get("stored_conversations") or ent.attributes.get("stored_embeddings"):
            factors.append("Stored data coupling.")

        count = len(set(factors))
        if count == 0:
            state = "LOW"
        elif count <= 2:
            state = "MODERATE"
        else:
            state = "HIGH"

        out.append(
            {
                "entity_id": ent.entity_id,
                "entity_type": ent.entity_type,
                "state": state,
                "factors": sorted(set(factors)),
                "limitations": [
                    "Lock-in is architectural/operational context, not inherently bad.",
                    "Migration difficulty requires substitutability and evaluation evidence.",
                ],
            }
        )

    return out


def analyze_changes(change_events: List[ChangeEvent]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    ordered = sorted(
        change_events,
        key=lambda x: x.effective_at or x.announced_at or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    flags: List[str] = []
    timeline: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

    for ch in ordered:
        timeline.append(
            {
                "change_id": ch.change_id,
                "entity_id": ch.entity_id,
                "change_type": ch.change_type.name,
                "effective_at": ch.effective_at,
                "announced_at": ch.announced_at,
                "old_state": ch.old_state,
                "new_state": ch.new_state,
                "impact_candidate": ch.impact_candidate,
                "evidence_ids": ch.evidence_ids,
                "limitations": ch.limitations,
            }
        )

        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.ALIAS_ROUTE_CHANGE:
            flags.append("ALIAS_ROUTE_CHANGE_REPRODUCIBILITY_RISK")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.MODEL_SWITCH:
            flags.append("MODEL_SWITCH_REQUIRES_EVALUATION")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.PRICING_CHANGE:
            flags.append("PRICING_CHANGE_TIME_BOUND_COST_CONTEXT")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.LICENSE_CHANGE:
            flags.append("LICENSE_CHANGE_REQUIRES_LEGAL_REVIEW")
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.GUARDRAIL_UPDATE:
            flags.append("GUARDRAIL_UPDATE_MAY_SHIFT_SAFETY_BEHAVIOR")

    return {
        "timeline": timeline,
        "flags": sorted(set(flags)),
        "limitations": [
            "Change is not automatically improvement.",
            "Behavior drift may arise from model, prompt, RAG, tool, guardrail, provider, or routing changes.",
        ],
    }


def analyze_source_independence(evidences: List[EvidenceRef]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    upstream_groups: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
    source_groups: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)

    for ev in evidences:
        upstream_groups[ev.upstream_source_id].append(ev.evidence_id)
        source_groups[ev.source_id].append(ev.evidence_id)

    dependent_roots = {root for root, ids in upstream_groups.items() if len(ids) > 1}

    if not upstream_groups:
        state = SourceIndependenceState.UNKNOWN
    elif not dependent_roots:
        state = SourceIndependenceState.INDEPENDENT
    elif len(dependent_roots) < len(upstream_groups):
        state = SourceIndependenceState.PARTIALLY_DEPENDENT
    else:
        state = SourceIndependenceState.DEPENDENT

    return {
        "state": state.name,
        "total_evidence_items": len(evidences),
        "unique_sources": len(source_groups),
        "unique_upstream_families": len(upstream_groups),
        "dependent_upstream_families": sorted(dependent_roots),
        "upstream_groups": {root: sorted(ids) for root, ids in upstream_groups.items()},
        "limitations": [
            "Multiple articles quoting one model card are not independent sources.",
            "Multiple leaderboards using the same evaluation harness/data are not independent evidence.",
        ],
    }


def detect_contradictions(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    alias_to_ids: Dict[str, Set[str]],
    entity_index: Dict[str, Entity],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_conflicts: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    pricing: List[Pricing],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    change_events: List[ChangeEvent],
    relationships: List[Relationship],
) -> List[Contradiction]:
    out: List[Contradiction] = []

    for rr in resolved_references:
        if rr.get("ambiguous"):
            out.append(
                Contradiction(
                    contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                    contradiction_type="REFERENCE_AMBIGUITY",
                    description=f"Reference '{rr.get('reference')}' resolved to multiple entities: {rr.get('resolved_ids')}.",
                    object_ids=rr.get("resolved_ids", []),
                    evidence_ids=rr.get("evidence_ids", []),
                    severity="MEDIUM",
                )
            )

    for alias, ids in alias_to_ids.items():
        model_ids = [iid for iid in ids if entity_index.get(iid) and entity_index[iid].entity_type == "MODEL"]
        if len(set(model_ids)) > 1:
            out.append(
                Contradiction(
                    contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                    contradiction_type="MODEL_ALIAS_AMBIGUITY",
                    description=f"Alias '{alias}' maps to multiple model identities: {sorted(set(model_ids))}.",
                    object_ids=sorted(set(model_ids)),
                    evidence_ids=[],
                    severity="HIGH",
                )
            )

    for conflict in benchmark_conflicts:
        out.append(
            Contradiction(
                contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                contradiction_type="BENCHMARK_SCORE_CONFLICT",
                description=conflict.get("description", "Benchmark protocol group produced conflicting scores."),
                object_ids=conflict.get("result_ids", []),
                evidence_ids=[],
                severity="MEDIUM",
            )
        )

    pricing_by_entity: Dict[str, List[Pricing]] = defaultdict(list)
    for p in pricing:
        if p.entity_id:
            pricing_by_entity[p.entity_id].append(p)

    for entity_id, items in pricing_by_entity.items():
        sorted_items = sorted(items, key=lambda x: x.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc))
        for i in range(len(sorted_items)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(sorted_items)):
                a = sorted_items[i]
                b = sorted_items[j]
                a_start = a.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                a_end = a.expiration_date or (a_start + timedelta(days=3650))
                b_start = b.effective_date or datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
                b_end = b.expiration_date or (b_start + timedelta(days=3650))

                if a_start <= b_end and b_start <= a_end:
                    if (
                        a.input_token_price_per_million is not None
                        and b.input_token_price_per_million is not None
                        and abs(a.input_token_price_per_million - b.input_token_price_per_million) > 1e-9
                    ):
                        out.append(
                            Contradiction(
                                contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                                contradiction_type="PRICING_OVERLAP_CONFLICT",
                                description=f"Overlapping pricing records for {entity_id} disagree on input token price.",
                                object_ids=[a.pricing_id, b.pricing_id],
                                evidence_ids=a.evidence_ids + b.evidence_ids,
                                severity="MEDIUM",
                            )
                        )

    for sm in security_mappings:
        if sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} and sm.get("base_model_impact") == "CANDIDATE_REQUIRES_VALIDATION":
            out.append(
                Contradiction(
                    contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                    contradiction_type="SECURITY_LAYER_CONFUSION_CANDIDATE",
                    description=f"Finding {sm.get('finding_id')} affects {sm.get('affected_layer')} but claims base-model vulnerability.",
                    object_ids=[sm.get("finding_id") or "", sm.get("affected_entity_id") or ""],
                    evidence_ids=[],
                    severity="HIGH",
                )
            )

    rel_by_source: Dict[str, List[Relationship]] = defaultdict(list)
    for r in relationships:
        rel_by_source[r.source_id].append(r)

    for ch in change_events:
        if ch.change_type == ChangeType.MODEL_SWITCH and ch.entity_id and ch.effective_at:
            old_model = None

            if isinstance(ch.old_state, dict):
                old_model = ch.old_state.get("model_id") or ch.old_state.get("value")
            else:
                old_model = ch.old_state

            if old_model:
                for r in rel_by_source.get(ch.entity_id, []):
                    if r.relation == "USES_MODEL" and r.target_id == old_model and (r.valid_to is None or r.valid_to > ch.effective_at):
                        out.append(
                            Contradiction(
                                contradiction_id=new_id("CONTRA"),
                                contradiction_type="DEPLOYMENT_STALE_MODEL_RELATIONSHIP_CANDIDATE",
                                description=(
                                    f"Change event indicates model switch for {ch.entity_id}, "
                                    f"but relationship to old model {old_model} remains active after effective time."
                                ),
                                object_ids=[ch.entity_id, old_model, r.relationship_id],
                                evidence_ids=ch.evidence_ids + r.evidence_ids,
                                severity="MEDIUM",
                            )
                        )

    return out


def clean_list(items: List[str]) -> List[str]:
    return [x for x in items if x]


def build_hypotheses(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    lineage: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    known_facts: Dict[str, Any],
) -> List[Hypothesis]:
    hypotheses: List[Hypothesis] = []

    alias_ambiguous = any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references)
    exact_version_resolved = any(rr.get("layer") == "MODEL" and not rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references)
    benchmark_limited = any(g.get("comparability_status") == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED.name for g in benchmark_groups.values())
    benchmark_conflict = any(g.get("conflict") for g in benchmark_groups.values())
    product_security = any(sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} for sm in security_mappings)
    incident_no_deployment = any(
        any(p.get("state") == "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE" for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    )
    common_mode = bool(supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"))
    alias_change = "ALIAS_ROUTE_CHANGE_REPRODUCIBILITY_RISK" in change_analysis.get("flags", [])

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H1",
            description="The referenced AI entity is resolved to an exact model/version/checkpoint.",
            support_evidence=clean_list(["Non-ambiguous model reference resolved." if exact_version_resolved else ""]),
            opposition_evidence=clean_list(["Alias/reference ambiguity detected." if alias_ambiguous else ""]),
            unknowns=["Exact checkpoint behind mutable API alias.", "Regional/provider routing variants."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if alias maps to multiple snapshots or provider routing is undisclosed.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.CANDIDATE if exact_version_resolved and not alias_ambiguous else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H2",
            description="Observed product/application capability is attributable to the base model alone.",
            support_evidence=clean_list(["No RAG/tool/guardrail dependencies observed."] if not common_mode else []),
            opposition_evidence=clean_list(["Product/deployment relationships include retrieval/tools/guardrails."] if common_mode or product_security else []),
            unknowns=["System prompt", "Tool permissions", "RAG corpus version", "Guardrail effectiveness."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if capability depends on retrieval, tools, prompts, or policy layers.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.REJECTED if common_mode or product_security else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H3",
            description="Benchmark scores are directly comparable across reported systems.",
            support_evidence=clean_list(["Complete protocol groups without conflict."] if not benchmark_limited and not benchmark_conflict else []),
            opposition_evidence=clean_list(["Protocol-limited groups."] if benchmark_limited else []),
            unknowns=["Prompting", "shots", "tools", "sampling", "evaluator", "benchmark version."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if protocol fields differ or scores conflict under same protocol.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.CANDIDATE if not benchmark_limited and not benchmark_conflict else HypothesisStatus.REJECTED,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H4",
            description="Security finding affects the base model rather than product/API/deployment layer.",
            support_evidence=[],
            opposition_evidence=clean_list(["Finding mapped to non-model layer."] if product_security else []),
            unknowns=["Model version", "deployment configuration", "guardrail layer", "tool authorization."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if advisory evidence ties issue to retrieval, prompt, tool, or runtime policy layer.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H5",
            description="Incident impacted all deployments using the referenced model.",
            support_evidence=[],
            opposition_evidence=clean_list(["No deployment evidence or version mismatch."] if incident_no_deployment else []),
            unknowns=["Exact deployed versions", "time windows", "regional routing."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if only specific versions/deployments were affected or inventory is missing.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H6",
            description="Multiple systems share common-mode AI dependencies creating correlated risk.",
            support_evidence=clean_list(["Shared provider/model/framework/vector-store/guardrail dependencies observed."] if common_mode else []),
            opposition_evidence=[],
            unknowns=["Hidden routing", "fallback models", "embedding migration constraints."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if dependencies are independent across version/provider/infrastructure.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.ACTIVE if common_mode else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    hypotheses.append(
        Hypothesis(
            id="H7",
            description="API alias route change may cause behavior or cost drift.",
            support_evidence=clean_list(["Alias route change event observed."] if alias_change else []),
            opposition_evidence=clean_list(["No alias change event."] if not alias_change else []),
            unknowns=["Routing policy", "snapshot equivalence", "evaluation delta."],
            falsification_criteria="Reject if alias route remained stable or new snapshot is proven equivalent.",
            status=HypothesisStatus.ACTIVE if alias_change else HypothesisStatus.INCONCLUSIVE,
        )
    )

    return [h for h in hypotheses if h.support_evidence or h.opposition_evidence or h.unknowns]


def skeptic_review(
    resolved_references: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    benchmark_groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]],
    security_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    incident_props: List[Dict[str, Any]],
    supply_chain: Dict[str, Any],
    change_analysis: Dict[str, Any],
    contradictions: List[Contradiction],
    known_facts: Dict[str, Any],
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    flags: List[str] = []
    alternatives: List[str] = []
    questions: List[str] = []

    if any(rr.get("ambiguous") for rr in resolved_references):
        flags.append("ENTITY_RESOLUTION_AMBIGUOUS")
        alternatives.append("Reference may denote product, API alias, deployment, or model family rather than exact checkpoint.")

    if any(g.get("comparability_status") == BenchmarkComparability.COMPARABILITY_LIMITED.name for g in benchmark_groups.values()):
        flags.append("BENCHMARK_COMPARABILITY_LIMITED")
        alternatives.append("Scores may use different prompting, shots, tools, sampling, evaluator, or benchmark version.")

    if any(g.get("conflict") for g in benchmark_groups.values()):
        flags.append("BENCHMARK_SCORE_CONFLICT")
        alternatives.append("Conflict may reflect non-independent republication, protocol drift, or measurement error.")

    if any(sm.get("affected_layer") not in {"MODEL", "CHECKPOINT"} for sm in security_mappings):
        flags.append("SECURITY_LAYER_NOT_MODEL")
        alternatives.append("Issue may reside in RAG, prompt, tool authorization, output handling, or runtime policy.")

    if any(
        any(p.get("state") == "NO_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE" for p in inc.get("deployment_propagation", []))
        for inc in incident_props
    ):
        flags.append("INCIDENT_DEPLOYMENT_SCOPE_UNRESOLVED")
        alternatives.append("Missing deployment inventory may hide affected systems.")

    if supply_chain.get("common_mode_dependencies"):
        flags.append("COMMON_MODE_DEPENDENCY")
        alternatives.append("Shared dependency may be intentional, contracted, or an artifact of incomplete inventory.")

    if change_analysis.get("flags"):
        flags.extend(change_analysis["flags"])
        alternatives.append("Behavior change may be operational rollout rather than degradation.")

    if contradictions:
        flags.append("CONTRADICTIONS_PRESENT")
        alternatives.append("Contradictions may reflect stale docs, alias routing, provider updates, or source dependency.")

    questions.extend(
        [
            "Did we separate model, product, API, deployment, and agent?",
            "Is the exact version/checkpoint resolved or only an alias?",
            "Are benchmark protocols normalized?",
            "Is capability provider-claimed or independently measured?",
            "Could RAG/tools/guardrails explain observed behavior?",
            "Does security finding affect base model or surrounding system?",
            "Are multiple sources actually independent?",
            "What evidence would disprove supply-chain or incident scope claims?",
        ]
    )

    return {
        "flags": sorted(set(flags)),
        "alternative_explanations": sorted(set(alternatives)),
        "diagnostic_questions": questions,
        "review_outcome": "PARTIAL_AGREEMENT" if flags else "AGREE",
        "boundary": "Defensive intelligence only. No model theft, prompt extraction, safeguard bypass, poisoning, or harmful agent enablement.",
    }


def build_facts(
